# Ekādasakanipāta (AN 11)

> **Nikāya:** Aṅguttaranikāya
> **Nipāta:** AN 11 (Ekādasakanipāta)
> **PTS Volume:** Aṅguttara Nikāya Vol. 5
> **Edition:** Pali Text Society (PTS)

---


<!-- PTS Page 181 -->
<a id="page-181"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 181]*

Ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī imehi dvīhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso, iminā ekena ṭhānena gārayho.

21. Tatra gahapati yvāyaṃ[^181-1] kāmabhogī dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasena, dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā asāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, saṃvibhajati puññāni karoti, te ca bhoge gadhito[^181-2] mucchito ajjhāpanno anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañño paribhuñjati: ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī tīhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso, ekena ṭhānena gārayho. Dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasenā ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, attānaṃ sukheti pīnetī ti iminā dutiyena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, saṃvibhajati puññāni karotī ti iminā tatiyena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, te ca bhoge gadhito mucchito ajjhāpanno anādīnavadassāvī anissaraṇapañño paribhuñjatī ti iminā ekena ṭhānena gārayho. Ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī imehi tīhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso, iminā ekena ṭhānena gārayho.

22. Tatra gahapati yvāyaṃ[^181-1] kāmabhogī dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasena, dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā asāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, saṃvibhajati puññāni karoti, te ca bhoge agadhito amucchito ajjhāpanno ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañño paribhuñjati: ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī catūhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso. Dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasenā[^181-3] ti iminā paṭhamena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, attānaṃ sukheti pīnetī ti iminā dutiyena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, saṃvibhajati puññāni karotī ti iminā tatiyena ṭhānena pāsaṃso, te ca bhoge agadhito amucchito anajjhāpanno ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañño paribhuñjatī[^181-4] ti iminā catutthena ṭhānena pāsaṃso. Ayaṃ gahapati kāmabhogī imehi catūhi ṭhānehi pāsaṃso. Ime kho gahapati dasa kāmabhogī[^181-5] santo saṃvijjamānā lokasmiṃ.

23. Imesaṃ kho gahapati dasannaṃ kāmabhogīnaṃ yvāyaṃ[^181-6] kāmabhogī dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasena,


<!-- PTS Page 182 -->
<a id="page-182"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 182]*

dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā asāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, saṃvibhajati puññāni karoti, te ca bhoge agadhito amucchito anajjhāpanno ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañño paribhuñjati: ayaṃ imesaṃ dasannaṃ kāmabhogīnaṃ aggo ca seṭṭho ca pāmokkho[^182-1] ca[^182-1] uttamo ca pavaro ca. Seyyathā pi gahapati gavā khīraṃ[^182-2] khīramhā dadhi dadhimhā navanītaṃ navanītamhā sappi sappimhā sappimaṇḍo tattha aggam akkhāyati, evam eva kho gahapati imesaṃ dasannaṃ kāmabhogīnaṃ yvāyaṃ[^182-3] kāmabhogī dhammena bhoge pariyesati asāhasena, dhammena bhoge pariyesitvā asāhasena attānaṃ sukheti pīneti, saṃvibhajati puññāni karoti, te ca bhoge agadhito[^182-4] amucchito anajjhāpanno ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañño paribhuñjati: ayaṃ imesaṃ dasannaṃ kāmabhogīnaṃ aggo ca seṭṭho ca pāmokkho[^182-5] ca uttamo ca pavaro cā ti. XCII.

1. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati yena Bhagavā . . .pe[^182-6] . . . Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Yato kho gahapati ariyasāvakassa pañca bhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti[^182-7], catūhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti, ariyo c'; assa ñāyo paññāya sudiṭṭho hoti suppaṭividdho, so ākaṅkhamāno attanā[^182-8] 'va[^182-8] attānaṃ vyākareyya[^182-9] ‘khīṇanirayo 'mhi khīṇatiracchānayoni[^182-10] khīṇapettivisayo[^182-11] khīṇāpāyaduggativinipāto, sotāpanno 'ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano'[^182-12] ti. Katamāni pañca bhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti?


<!-- PTS Page 183 -->
<a id="page-183"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 183]*

3. Yaṃ gahapati pāṇātipātī pāṇātipātapaccayā diṭṭhadhammikam pi bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, samparāyikam pi bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, cetasikam pi dukkhaṃ domanassaṃ paṭisaṃvedeti; pāṇātipātā paṭivirato neva diṭṭhadhammikaṃ bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, na samparāyikaṃ bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, na cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ domanassaṃ paṭisaṃvedeti. Pāṇātipātā paṭiviratassa evan taṃ bhayaṃ veraṃ vūpasantaṃ hoti.

4. Yaṃ gahapati adinnādāyī . . . pe[^183-1] . . . kāmesu micchācārī . . . musāvādī . . .[^183-2] surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhāyī surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānapaccayā diṭṭhadhammikam pi bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, samparāyikam pi bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, cetasikam pi dukkhaṃ domanassaṃ paṭisaṃvedeti; surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭivirato neva diṭṭhadhammikaṃ bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, na samparāyikaṃ bhayaṃ veraṃ pasavati, na cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ domanassaṃ paṭisaṃvedeti. Surāmerayamajjapamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratassa evan taṃ bhayaṃ veraṃ vūpasantaṃ hoti. Imāni pañca bhayāni verāni vūpasantani honti. Katamehi catūhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti?

5. Idha gahapati ariyasāvako buddhe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti ‘iti pi so Bhagavā arahaṃ[^183-3] sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā'; ti. Dhamme aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti ‘svākkhāto Bhagavatā dhammo sandiṭṭhiko akāliko ehipassiko opanayiko[^183-4] paccattaṃ veditabbo viññūhī'; ti. Saṅghe aveccappasādena samannāgato hoti ‘supaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, ujupaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, ñāyapaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, sāmīcipaṭipanno Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho, yad idaṃ cattāri purisayugāni, aṭṭha purisapuggalā, esa Bhagavato sāvakasaṅgho āhuneyyo pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā'; ti. Ariyakantehi sīlehi samannāgato


<!-- PTS Page 184 -->
<a id="page-184"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 184]*

hoti akhaṇḍehi acchiddehi asabalehi[^184-1] akammāsehi bhujissehi[^184-2] viññūppasatthehi[^184-3] aparāmaṭṭhehi samādhisaṃvattanikehi. Imehi catūhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti. Katamo c'; assa ariyo ñāyo paññāya sudiṭṭho hoti supaṭividdho?

6. Idha gahapati ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati: Iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hoti, imass'; uppādā idaṃ uppajjati, imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hoti, imassa nirodhā idaṃ nirujjhati. Yad idaṃ avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ, viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapaccayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapaccayā phasso, phassapaccayā vedanā, vedanāpaccayā taṇhā, taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ, upādānapaccayā bhavo, bhavapaccayā jāti, jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇaṃ sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā sambhavanti. Evam etassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayo hoti. Avijjāya tveva asesavirāganirodhā saṅkhāranirodho[^184-4], saṅkhāranirodhā viññāṇanirodho, viññāṇanirodhā nāmarūpanirodho, nāmarūpanirodhā saḷāyatananirodho, saḷāyatananirodhā phassanirodho, phassanirodhā vedanānirodho, vedanānirodhā taṇhānirodho, taṇhānirodhā upādānanirodho, upādānanirodhā bhavanirodho, bhavanirodhā jātinirodho, jātinirodhā jarāmaraṇaṃ sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti. Evam etassa kevalassa dukkhakkhandhassa nirodho hoti, ayañ c'; assa[^184-5] ariyo ñāyo paññāya sudiṭṭho hoti suppaṭividdho. Yato kho gahapati ariyasāvakassa imāni pañca bhayāni verāni vūpasantāni honti, imehi catūhi sotāpattiyaṅgehi samannāgato hoti, ayañ[^184-6] c'; assa[^184-6] ariyo ñāyo paññāya sudiṭṭho hoti suppaṭividdho, so[^184-7] ākaṅkhamāno attanā 'va attānaṃ vyākareyya[^184-8] ‘khīṇanirayo 'mhi khīṇatiracchānayoni[^184-8] khīṇapettivisayo[^184-10] khīṇāpāyaduggativinipāto, sotāpanno 'ham asmi avinipātadhammo niyato sambodhiparāyano'; ti.


<!-- PTS Page 185 -->
<a id="page-185"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 185]*

XCIII.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati divādivassa Sāvatthiyā nikkhami Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa etad ahosi ‘akālo kho tāva Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, paṭisallīno Bhagavā, manobhāvanīyānam[^185-1] pi[^185-1] bhikkhūnaṃ akālo dassanāya, paṭisallīnā manobhāvanīyā bhikkhū, yan nūnāhaṃ yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten' upasaṅkameyyan'; ti. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkami.

2. Tena kho pana samayena aññatitthiyā paribbājakā saṅgamma samāgamma[^185-2] unnādino uccāsaddā mahāsaddā anekavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathentā nisinnā honti. Addasaṃsu[^185-3] kho te aññatitthiyā paribbājakā Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ dūrato 'va āgacchantaṃ, disvāna[^185-4] aññamaññaṃ saṇṭhāpesuṃ[^185-5]: ‘Appasaddā bhonto hontu, mā bhonto saddam akattha. Ayaṃ Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati āgacchati samaṇassa Gotamassa sāvako[^185-6]. Yāvatā kho pana samaṇassa Gotamassa sāvakā gihī odātavasanā[^185-7] Sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasanti[^185-8], ayaṃ tesaṃ aññataro Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati. Appasaddakāmā kho pana te āyasmanto appasaddavinītā appasaddassa vaṇṇavādino, app eva nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ maññeyyā' ti. Atha kho te paribbājakā tuṇhī ahesuṃ.

3. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati yena te paribbājakā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 186 -->
<a id="page-186"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 186]*

sārāṇīyaṃ[^186-1] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ te[^186-2] paribbājakā etad avocuṃ ‘vadehi gahapati kiṃdiṭṭhiko samaṇo Gotamo' ti. ‘Na kho ahaṃ bhante Bhagavato sabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jānāmī'; ti. ‘Iti[^186-3] kira tvaṃ gahapati na samaṇassa Gotamassa sabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jānāsi, vadehi gahapati kiṃdiṭṭhikā bhikkhū'; ti. ‘Bhikkhūnaṃ pi kho ahaṃ bhante na sabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jānāmī'; ti. ‘Iti kira tvaṃ gahapati na samaṇassa Gotamassa sabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jānāsi, na pi bhikkhūnaṃ sabbaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jānāsi, vadehi gahapati kiṃdiṭṭhiko 'si tuvan'[^186-4] ti. ‘Etaṃ kho bhante amhehi na dukkaraṃ vyākātuṃ[^186-5] yaṃdiṭṭhikā mayan ti, iṅgha tāva āyasmanto[^186-6] yathā sakāni diṭṭhigatāni vyākarontu[^186-5], pacchā p'; etaṃ[^186-7] amhehi no dukkaraṃ bhavissati vyākātuṃ[^186-5] yaṃdiṭṭhikā mayan'; ti.

4. Evaṃ vutte aññataro paribbājako Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ etad avoca ‘sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃdiṭṭiko[^186-8] ahaṃ gahapatī'; ti. Aññataro pi kho paribbājako Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ etad avoca ‘asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃdiṭṭhiko ahaṃ gahapatī'; ti. Aññataro pi kho paribbājako Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ etad avoca ‘antavā loko . . .[^186-9] anantavā[^186-10] loko[^186-10] . . . taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ . . . aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ . . . hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . na[^186-10] hoti[^186-10] Tathāgato[^186-10] parammaraṇā[^186-10] . . . hoti cana ca[^186-11] hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃdiṭṭhiko ahaṃ gahapatī'; ti.

5. Evaṃ vutte Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati te paribbājake etad avoca:-- Yvāyaṃ[^186-12] bhante āyasmā evam āha ‘sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃdiṭṭhiko ahaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 187 -->
<a id="page-187"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 187]*

gahapatī'; ti, imassa[^187-1] ayam[^187-1] āyasmato diṭṭhi attano vā ayonisomanasikārahetu uppannā paraghosapaccayā vā. Sā kho pan'; esā diṭṭhi bhūtā saṃkhatā cetayitā[^187-2] paṭiccasamuppannā[^187-3]; yaṃ kho pana kiñci bhūtaṃ saṃkhatam cetayitaṃ paṭiccasamuppannaṃ, tad aniccaṃ, yad[^187-4] aniccaṃ[^187-4], taṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tad eva[^187-5] so[^187-5] āyasmā allīno, tad eva so āyasmā ajjhūpagato. Yo p'; āyaṃ bhante āyasmā evam āha ‘asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃdiṭṭhiko ahaṃ gahapatī'; ti, imassa pi ayam āyasmato diṭṭhi attano vā ayonisomanasikārahetu uppannā paraghosapaccayā vā. Sā kho pan'; esā diṭṭhi bhūtā saṃkhatā cetayitā paṭiccasamuppannā; yaṃ kho pana kiñci bhūtaṃ saṃkhataṃ cetayitaṃ paṭiccasamuppannaṃ, tad aniccaṃ, yad aniccaṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tad eva so āyasmā allīno, tad eva so āyasmā ajjhūpagato. Yo p'; āyaṃ bhante āyasmā evam āha ‘antavā loko . . .[^187-6] anantavā loko . . . taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ . . . aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ . . . hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . hoti ca[^187-7] na ca[^187-8] hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃdiṭṭhiko ahaṃ gahapatī'; ti imassa pi ayam āyasmato diṭṭhi attano vā ayonisomanasikārahetu uppannā paraghosapaccayā vā. Sā kho pan'; esā diṭṭhi bhūtā saṃkhatā cetayitā paṭiccasamuppannā; yaṃ kho pana kiñci bhūtaṃ saṃkhataṃ cetayitaṃ[^187-9] paṭiccasamuppannaṃ, tad aniccaṃ, yad aniccaṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tad eva so āyasmā allīno, tad eva so āyasmā ajjhūpagato ti.


<!-- PTS Page 188 -->
<a id="page-188"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 188]*

6. Evaṃ vutte te paribbājakā Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ etad avocuṃ ‘vyākatāni[^188-1] kho gahapati amhehi sabbeh'; eva yathā sakāni diṭṭhigatāni, vadehi gahapati kiṃdiṭṭhiko 'si tuvan'[^188-2] ti. ‘Yaṃ kho[^188-3] bhante kiñci bhūtaṃ saṃkhataṃ cetayitaṃ[^188-4] paṭiccasamuppannaṃ, tad aniccaṃ, yad aniccaṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ dukkhaṃ, taṃ "n'; etaṃ mama n'; eso 'ham asmi na me so attā" ti evaṃdiṭṭhiko kho ahaṃ bhante'; ti. ‘Yaṃ kho gahapati kiñci bhūtaṃ saṃkhataṃ cetayitaṃ paṭiccasamuppannaṃ, tad aniccaṃ, yad aniccaṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tad eva tvaṃ gahapati allīno, tad eva tvaṃ gahapati ajjhūpagato'; ti. ‘Yaṃ kho pana bhante kiñci bhūtaṃ saṃkhataṃ cetayitaṃ paṭiccasamuppannaṃ, tad aniccaṃ, yad aniccaṃ, taṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ dukkhaṃ, taṃ "n'; etaṃ mama n'; eso 'ham asmi na m'; eso attā" ti: evam etaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya sudiṭṭhaṃ, tassa ca uttariṃ[^188-5] nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāmī'; ti. Evaṃ vutte te paribbājakā tuṇhībhūtā maṅkubhūtā pattakkhandhā adhomukhā pajjhāyantā appaṭibhānā[^188-6] nisīdiṃsu.

7. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati te paribbājake tuṇhībhūte maṅkubhūte pattakkhandhe adhomukhe pajjhāyante appaṭibhāne viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati yāvatako ahosi tehi aññatitthiyehi[^188-7] paribbājakehi saddhiṃ kathāsallāpo, taṃ sabbaṃ Bhagavato ārocesi. ‘Sādhu sādhu gahapati, evaṃ kho te gahapati moghapurisā kālena kālaṃ saha dhammena suniggahītaṃ niggahetabbā'; ti. Atha kho Bhagavā Anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassesi samādapesi samuttejesi sampahaṃsesi. Atha kho Anāthapiṇḍiko gahapati Bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassito samādapito samuttejito sampahaṃsito


<!-- PTS Page 189 -->
<a id="page-189"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 189]*

uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi.

8. Atha kho Bhagavā acirapakkante Anāthapiṇḍike gahapatimhi bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Yo pi so[^189-1] bhikkhave bhikkhu vassasatupasampanno[^189-2] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye, so pi evam evaṃ aññatitthiye paribbājake saha dhammena suniggahītaṃ niggaṇheyya[^189-3], yathā taṃ Anāthapiṇḍikena gahapatinā niggahītā ti. XCIV.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ Bhagavā Campāyaṃ viharati Gaggarāya pokkharaṇiyā tīre. Atha kho Vajjiyamāhito[^189-3] gahapati divādivassa Campāya nikkhami Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya. Atha kho Vajjiyamāhitassa[^189-5] gahapatissa etad ahosi ‘akālo kho tāva Bhagavantaṃ dassanāya, paṭisallīno Bhagavā, manobhāvanīyānaṃ pi[^189-6] bhikkhūnaṃ akālo dassanāya, paṭisallīnā manobhāvanīyā[^189-7] bhikkhū, yan nūnāhaṃ yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkameyyan' ti. Atha kho Vajjiyamāhito[^189-8] gahapati yena aññatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmo ten'; upasaṅkami.

2. Tena kho pana samayena aññatitthiyā[^189-9] paribbājakā saṅgamma samāgamma[^189-10] unnādino uccāsaddā mahāsaddā anekavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathentā nisinnā honti. Addasaṃsu kho te aññatitthiyā paribbājakā Vajjiyamāhitaṃ[^189-11] gahapatiṃ dūrato 'va āgacchantaṃ, disvāna[^189-12] aññamaññaṃ saṇṭhāpesuṃ[^189-13]: ‘Appasaddā bhonto hontu, mā bhonto saddam akattha. Ayaṃ Vajjiyamāhito gahapati āgacchati samaṇassa Gotamassa sāvako. Yāvatā[^189-14] kho


<!-- PTS Page 190 -->
<a id="page-190"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 190]*

pana samaṇassa Gotamassa[^190-1] sāvakā gihī odātavasanā Campāyaṃ paṭivasanti, ayaṃ tesaṃ aññataro Vajjiyamāhito gahapati. Appasaddakāmā kho pana te āyasmanto appasaddavinītā appasaddassa vaṇṇavādino. App eva nāma appasaddaṃ parisaṃ viditvā upasaṅkamitabbaṃ maññeyyā' ti. Atha kho te paribbājakā tuṇhī ahesuṃ.

3. Atha kho Vajjiyamāhito[^190-2] gahapati yena paribbājakā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^190-3] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Vajjiyamāhitaṃ gahapatiṃ te paribbājakā etad avocuṃ ‘saccaṃ kira gahapati, samaṇo Gotamo sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahati, sabbaṃ tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ ekaṃsena upakkosati upavadatī'; ti? ‘Na kho bhante[^190-4] Bhagavā sabbaṃ tapaṃ garahati, na pi sabbaṃ tapassiṃ lūkhājīviṃ ekaṃsena upakkosati upavadati. Gārayhaṃ kho[^190-5] bhante Bhagavā garahati, pasaṃsiyaṃ[^190-6] pasaṃsati, gārayhaṃ kho pana bhante Bhagavā garahanto pasaṃsiyaṃ[^190-6] pasaṃsanto vibhajjavādo[^190-7] Bhagavā, na so Bhagavā ettha ekaṃsavādo'[^190-7] ti.

4. Evaṃ vutte aññataro paribbājako Vajjiyamāhitaṃ[^190-8] gahapatiṃ etad avoca ‘āgamehi tvaṃ[^190-9] gahapati[^190-10], yassa tvaṃ samaṇassa Gotamassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsasi, so[^190-11] samaṇo Gotamo venayiko appaññattiko'; ti? ‘Ettha p'; āhaṃ bhante āyasmante vakkhāmi saha dhammena: idaṃ kusalan ti bhante Bhagavatā paññattaṃ, idaṃ akusalan ti bhante Bhagavatā[^190-12] paññattaṃ[^190-12]. Iti kusalākusalaṃ[^190-13] Bhagavā paññāpayamāno[^190-14] sappaññattiko Bhagavā[^190-15], na so Bhagavā venayiko appaññattiko'[^190-16] ti. Evaṃ vutte te[^190-15] paribbājakā


<!-- PTS Page 191 -->
<a id="page-191"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 191]*

tuṇhībhūtā maṅkubhūtā pattakkhandhā adhomukhā pajjhāyantā appaṭibhānā nisīdiṃsu.

5. Atha kho Vajjiyamāhito[^191-1] gahapati te paribbājake tuṇhībhūte maṅkubhūte pattakkhandhe adhomukhe pajjhāyante appaṭibhāne viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Vajjiyamāhito[^191-2] gahapati yāvatako ahosi tehi aññatitthiyehi paribbājakehi saddhiṃ kathāsallāpo, taṃ sabbaṃ Bhagavato ārocesi.

6. Sādhu sādhu gahapati, evaṃ kho te gahapati moghapurisā kālena kālaṃ saha dhammena suniggahītaṃ niggahetabbā. Nāhaṃ gahapati sabbaṃ tapaṃ tapitabban ti vadāmi. Na[^191-3] panāhaṃ[^191-4] gahapati sabbaṃ tapaṃ na tapitabban ti vadāmi. Nāhaṃ gahapati sabbaṃ samādānaṃ samāditabban ti vadāmi. Na panāhaṃ gahapati sabbaṃ[^191-5] samādānaṃ na samāditabban ti vadāmi. Nāhaṃ gahapati sabbaṃ padhānaṃ padahitabban ti vadāmi. Na panāhaṃ gahapati sabbaṃ padhānaṃ na padahitabban ti vadāmi. Nāhaṃ gahapati sabbo[^191-6] paṭinissaggo[^191-7] paṭinissajjitabbo[^191-8] ti vadāmi. Na[^191-9] panāhaṃ gahapati sabbo[^191-10] paṭinissaggo[^191-11] na paṭinissajjitabbo[^191-8] ti vadāmi. Nāhaṃ gahapati sabbā[^191-12] vimutti[^191-13] vimuccitabbā[^191-14] ti vadāmi. Na panāhaṃ gahapati sabbā[^191-12] vimutti[^191-13] na vimuccitabbā ti vadāmi.

7. Yaṃ hi gahapati tapaṃ tapato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, evarūpaṃ tapaṃ na tapitabban ti vadāmi. Yañ ca khvāssa[^191-15] gahapati[^191-16] tapaṃ tapato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā


<!-- PTS Page 192 -->
<a id="page-192"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 192]*

dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, evarūpaṃ tapaṃ tapitabban ti vadāmi. Yaṃ hi gahapati samādānaṃ samādiyato[^192-1] akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, evarūpaṃ samādānaṃ na samāditabban ti vadāmi. Yañ ca khvāssa gahapati samādānaṃ samādiyato[^192-1] akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, evarūpaṃ samādānaṃ samāditabban ti vadāmi. Yaṃ hi gahapati padhānaṃ padahato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, evarūpaṃ padhānaṃ na padahitabban ti vadāmi. Yañ ca khvāssa gahapati padhānaṃ padahato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, evarūpaṃ padhānaṃ padahitabban ti vadāmi. Yaṃ hi gahapati paṭinissaggaṃ paṭinissajjato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, evarūpo paṭinissaggo na paṭinissajjitabbo ti vadāmi. Yañ ca khvāssa[^192-2] gahapati paṭinissaggaṃ paṭinissajjato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, evarūpo paṭinissaggo paṭinissajjitabbo ti vadāmi. Yaṃ hi gahapati vimuttiṃ vimuccato akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, evarūpā vimutti na vimuccitabbā ti vadāmi. Yañ ca khvāssa[^192-2] gahapati vimuttiṃ vimuccato akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, evarūpā vimutti vimuccitabbā ti vadāmī ti. Atha kho Vajjiyamāhito[^192-3] gahapati Bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassito samādapito samuttejito sampahaṃsito uṭṭhāyāsanā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmi.

8. Atha kho Bhagavā acirapakkante Vajjiyamāhite[^192-3] gahapatimhi bhikkhū āmantesi:-- Yo pi so[^192-4] bhikkhu dīgharattaṃ apparajakkho[^192-5] imasmiṃ dhammavinaye, so pi evam evaṃ[^192-6] aññatitthiye paribbājake saha dhammena suniggahītaṃ niggaṇheyya[^192-7], yathā taṃ Vajjiyamāhitena[^192-3] gahapatinā niggahītā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 193 -->
<a id="page-193"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 193]*

XCV.

1. Atha kho Uttiyo paribbājako yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Uttiyo paribbājako Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘kin nu kho bho Gotama sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti? ‘Avyākataṃ kho etaṃ[^193-1] Uttiya mayā: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti. ‘Kiṃ pana bho Gotama asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti? ‘Etam pi kho Uttiya avyākataṃ mayā: asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti. ‘Kin nu kho bho Gotama antavā loko[^193-2] . . .[^193-3] anantavā[^193-4] loko[^193-4] . . . taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ . . . aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ . . . hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . hoti[^193-5] ca na ca hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti? ‘Etam pi kho Uttiya avyākataṃ mayā: neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti.

2. ‘Kin nu kho bho Gotama sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘avyākataṃ kho etaṃ Uttiya mayā: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti vadesi. ‘Kiṃ pana bho Gotama asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘etam pi kho Uttiya avyākataṃ mayā: asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti vadesi. ‘Kin nu kho bho Gotama antavā loko . . .[^193-3] anantavā[^193-6] loko[^193-6] . . . taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ . . . aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ . . ḥoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . hoti ca na ca hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti iti puṭṭho


<!-- PTS Page 194 -->
<a id="page-194"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 194]*

samāno ‘etam pi kho Uttiya avyākataṃ mayā: neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti vadesi. ‘Atha kiñcarahi[^194-1] bhotā Gotamena vyākatan'; ti? ‘Abhiññāya[^194-2] kho[^194-3] ahaṃ Uttiya sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ desemi sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya[^194-4] ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāyā' ti. ‘Yaṃ pan'; etaṃ[^194-5] bhavaṃ Gotamo abhiññāya[^194-6] sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ desesi[^194-7] sattānaṃ visuddhiyā sokaparidevānaṃ[^194-8] samatikkamāya dukkhadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya[^194-4] ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, sabbo ca[^194-9] tena loko niyyissati[^194-10] upaḍḍho[^194-11] vā tibhāgo[^194-12] vā'; ti? [^194-13] Evaṃ vutte Bhagavā tuṇhī ahosi.

3. Atha kho āyasmato Ānandassa etad ahosi ‘mā h'; evaṃ[^194-14] kho Uttiyo paribbājako pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ paṭilabhati[^194-15]: sabbasāmukkaṃsikaṃ[^194-16] vata me[^194-17] samaṇo Gotamo pañhaṃ puṭṭho saṃsādeti[^194-18] no vissajjeti na[^194-19] nūna visahatī ti, tad[^194-20] assa[^194-20] Uttiyassa paribbājakassa dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā'; ti. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando Uttiyaṃ paribbājakaṃ etad avoca:--

4. ‘Tena[^194-21] h'; āvuso[^194-21] Uttiya upaman te karissāmi, upamāyam[^194-22] idh'; ekacce viññū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānanti. Seyyathā pi āvuso Uttiya rañño paccantimaṃ nagaraṃ daḷhuddāpaṃ[^194-23] daḷhapākāratoraṇaṃ ekadvāraṃ. Tatr' assa dovāriko paṇḍito vyatto medhāvī aññātānaṃ[^194-24] nivāretā


<!-- PTS Page 195 -->
<a id="page-195"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 195]*

ñātānaṃ pavesetā, so tassa nagarassa samantā anupariyāyapathaṃ anukkamamāno[^195-1] na passeyya pākārasandhiṃ[^195-2] vā pākāravivaraṃ vā antamaso biḷāranissakkanamattaṃ[^195-3] pi, no[^195-4] ca[^195-4] khvāssa[^195-5] evaṃ ñāṇaṃ hoti ‘ettakā pāṇā imaṃ nagaraṃ pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā'[^195-6] ti[^195-6]. Atha khvāssa[^195-5] evam ettha hoti ‘ye kho[^195-6] keci oḷārikā pāṇā imaṃ nagaraṃ pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā, sabbe te iminā dvārena pavisanti vā nikkhamanti vā'; ti. Evam eva kho āvuso Uttiya[^195-7] na[^195-8] Tathāgatassa evaṃ[^195-9] ussukkataṃ[^195-10] hoti ‘sabbo ca[^195-11] tena loko niyyissati[^195-12] upaḍḍho vā tibhāgo vā'; ti. Atha kho evam ettha Tathāgatassa hoti ‘ye kho keci lokamhā niyyiṃsu[^195-13] vā niyyanti vā niyyissanti[^195-14] vā, sabbe te pañca nīvaraṇe pahāya cetaso upakkilese paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe catūsu satipaṭṭhānesu supatiṭṭhitacittā[^195-15] satta bojjhaṅge yathābhūtaṃ bhāvetvā evam ete[^195-16] lokamhā niyyiṃsu[^195-13] vā niyyanti vā niyyissanti[^195-17] vā'; ti. Yad eva kho[^195-18] tvaṃ[^195-18] āvuso Uttiya Bhagavantaṃ[^195-19] pañhaṃ apucchi[^195-20], tad eva[^195-21] taṃ[^195-6] pañhaṃ Bhagavantaṃ aññena pariyāyena apucchi. Tasmā te[^195-22] taṃ[^195-22] Bhagavā[^195-23] na vyākāsī[^195-24] ti.


<!-- PTS Page 196 -->
<a id="page-196"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 196]*

XCVI.

1. Ekaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā Ānando Rājagahe[^196-1] viharati Tapodārāme. Atha kho āyasmā Ānando rattiyā paccūsasamayaṃ paccuṭṭhāya yena Tapodā ten'; upasaṅkami gattāni parisiñcituṃ. Tapodāya[^196-2] gattāni parisiñcitvā paccuttaritvā ekacīvaro aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamāno[^196-3]. Kokanudo[^196-4] pi kho paribbājako rattiyā paccūsasamayaṃ paccuṭṭhāya yena Tapodā ten'; upasaṅkami gattāni parisiñcituṃ. Addasā[^196-5] kho Kokanudo paribbājako āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ dūrato 'va āgacchantaṃ, disvāna[^196-6] āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ etad avoca ‘kvattha[^196-7] āvuso'; ti? ‘Amhāvuso[^196-8] bhikkhū'; ti. ‘Katamesaṃ[^196-9] āvuso bhikkhūnan'; ti? ‘Samaṇānaṃ āvuso Sakyaputtiyānan'; ti. ‘Puccheyyāma[^196-10] mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ kiñci-d-eva desaṃ, sace āyasmā okāsaṃ karoti pañhassa veyyākaraṇāyā'; ti. ‘Pucchāvuso, sutvā[^196-11] vedissāmā'; ti.

2. ‘Kin nu kho bho[^196-12]: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃdiṭṭhi[^196-13] bhavan'; ti? ‘Na kho ahaṃ āvuso evaṃdiṭṭhi: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti. ‘Kiṃ pana[^196-14] bho: asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃ diṭṭhi bhavan'; ti? ‘Na kho ahaṃ āvuso evaṃdiṭṭhi: asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti. ‘Kin nu kho bho: antavā loko . . .[^196-15] anantavā loko . . . taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ . . . aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ . . . hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . hoti ca na ca


<!-- PTS Page 197 -->
<a id="page-197"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 197]*

hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃdiṭṭhi bhavan'; ti? ‘Na kho ahaṃ āvuso evaṃdiṭṭhi: neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti. ‘Tena hi bhavaṃ na jānāti na passatī'; ti? ‘Na kho ahaṃ āvuso na jānāmi na passāmi, jānām'; ahaṃ[^197-1] āvuso passāmī'; ti.

3. ‘Kin nu kho bho: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃdiṭṭhi bhavan'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘na kho ahaṃ āvuso evaṃdiṭṭhi: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, moghaṃ aññan'; ti vadesi. ‘Kiṃ[^197-2] pana[^197-3] bho: asassato loko, idaṃ eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti evaṃdiṭṭhi bhavan'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘na kho ahaṃ āvuso evaṃdiṭṭhi: asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti vadesi. ‘Kin nu kho bho: antavā loko . . .[^197-4] anantavā loko . . . taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ . . . aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ . . . hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . hoti ca na ca hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti evaṃdiṭṭhi bhavan'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘na kho ahaṃ āvuso evaṃdiṭṭhi: neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti vadesi. ‘Tena hi bhavaṃ na jānāti na passatī'; ti iti puṭṭho samāno ‘na kho ahaṃ āvuso na jānāmi na passāmi, jānām'; ahaṃ āvuso passāmī'; ti vadesi. ‘Yathākathaṃ panāvuso imassa bhāsitassa attho daṭṭhabbo'; ti?

4. ‘Sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti kho āvuso diṭṭhigatam etaṃ, ‘asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan'; ti kho āvuso diṭṭhigatam etaṃ, ‘antavā loko . . .[^197-4] anantavā loko . . . taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ . . . aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ . . . hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . hoti ca na ca hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā . . . neva hoti na na hoti Tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ,


<!-- PTS Page 198 -->
<a id="page-198"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 198]*

mogham aññan'; ti kho āvuso diṭṭhigatam etaṃ. Yāvatā āvuso diṭṭhigatā, yāvatā diṭṭhiṭṭhāna-adhiṭṭhāna-pariyuṭṭhāna[^198-1]-samuṭṭhāna[^198-2]-samugghāto[^198-3], tam ahaṃ jānāmi tam ahaṃ passāmi[^198-4], tam ahaṃ jānanto[^198-5] tam[^198-6] ahaṃ[^198-6] passanto[^198-7] kyāhaṃ[^198-8] vakkhāmi ‘na jānāmi na passāmī'; ti[^198-9]? Jānām'; ahaṃ āvuso passāmī ti. ‘Ko nāmo[^198-10] āyasmā, kathañ ca panāyasmantaṃ sabrahmacārī jānantī'[^198-11] ti? Ānando ti kho me āvuso nāmaṃ, Ānando ti ca pana maṃ sabrahmacārī jānantī[^198-11] ti. ‘Mahācariyena vata[^198-12] kira bhotā[^198-13] saddhiṃ mantayamānā[^198-14] na jānimha[^198-15]: āyasmā Ānando ti. Sace hi mayaṃ sañjāneyyāma[^198-16]: āyasmā[^198-17] Ānando ti, ettakam pi no na ppaṭibhāseyya[^198-18], khamatu ca me āyasmā Ānando'; ti. XCVII.

1. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti[^198-12] pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassa[^198-19]. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, anumattesu[^198-20] vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu.

3. Bahussuto hoti sutadharo sutasannicayo, ye te dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhe kalyāṇā pariyosānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 199 -->
<a id="page-199"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 199]*

savyañjanaṃ kevalapuripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpassa[^199-1] dhammā bahussutā honti dhatā[^199-2] vacasā paricitā manasānupekkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā.

4. Kalyāṇamitto hoti kalyāṇasahāyo kalyāṇasampavaṅko[^199-3].

5. Sammādiṭṭhiko hoti sammādassanena samannāgato.

6. Anekavihitaṃ iddhividdhaṃ paccanubhoti: eko pi hutvā bahudhā hoti, bahudhā pi hutvā eko hoti, āvibhāvaṃ tirobhāvaṃ tirokuḍḍaṃ tiropākāraṃ tiropabbataṃ asajjamāno gacchati, seyyathā pi ākāse, paṭhaviyā pi ummujjanimmujjaṃ karoti, seyyathā pi udake, udake pi abhijjamāne gacchati, seyyathā pi paṭhaviyaṃ, ākāse pi pallaṅkena kamati, seyyathā pi pakkhī sakuṇo, ime pi candimasuriye evaṃmahiddhike evaṃmahānubhāve pāṇinā parāmasati[^199-4] parimajjati, yāva Brahmalokā pi[^199-5] kāyena 'va saṃvatteti.

7. Dibbāya sotadhātuyā visuddhāya atikkantamānusikāya[^199-6] ubho sadde suṇāti dibbe ca mānuse ca ye dūre santike ca.

8. Parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ cetasā ceto paricca pajānāti: sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ cittan'; ti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītarāgaṃ cittan'; ti pajānāti, sadosaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . pe[^199-7] . . . vītadosaṃ vā cittaṃ . . .[^199-8] samohaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . vītamohaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ . . . amahaggataṃ[^199-9] vā cittaṃ . . . mahaggataṃ[^199-9] vā cittaṃ . . . sa-uttaraṃ vā cittaṃ . . . anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ . . . asamāhitaṃ[^199-10] vā cittaṃ . . . samāhitaṃ[^199-10] vā cittaṃ . . . avimuttaṃ[^199-11] vā cittaṃ . . . vimuttaṃ[^199-11] vā cittaṃ ‘vimuttaṃ cittan'; ti pajānāti.

9. Anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ‘ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo tisso pi jātiyo catasso pi jātiyo


<!-- PTS Page 200 -->
<a id="page-200"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 200]*

pañca pi jātiyo dasa pi jātiyo vīsam[^200-1] pi[^200-2] jātiyo tiṃsam pi jātiyo cattālīsam[^200-3] pi jātiyo paṇṇāsam pi jātiyo jātisatam pi jātisahassam pi jātisatasahassam pi aneke pi saṃvaṭṭakappe aneke pi vivaṭṭakappe aneke pi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappe amutrāsiṃ evaṃnāmo evaṃgotto evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvedī evamāyupariyanto, so tato cuto amutra udapādiṃ[^200-4], tatra[^200-5] p'; āsiṃ[^200-5] evaṃnāmo evaṃgotto evaṃvaṇṇo evamāhāro evaṃsukhadukkhapaṭisaṃvedī evamāyupariyanto, so tato cuto idh'; upapanno'[^200-6] ti: iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati.

10. Dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena[^200-7] satte passati cavamāne upapajjamāne[^200-8] hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammūpage satte pajānāti ‘ime vata[^200-9] bhonto sattā kāyaduccaritena samannāgatā vacīduccaritena[^200-10] samannāgatā manoduccaritena samannāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapannā[^200-11], ime vā pana bhonto sattā kāyasucaritena samannāgatā vacīsucaritena[^200-12] samannāgatā manosucaritena samannāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, te kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapannā'[^200-11] ti: iti dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena satte passati cavamāne upapajjamāne[^200-8] hīne paṇīte suvaṇṇe dubbaṇṇe sugate duggate yathākammūpage satte pajānāti.

11. Āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati.


<!-- PTS Page 201 -->
<a id="page-201"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 201]*

Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgato bhikkhu āhuneyyo hoti[^201-1] pāhuneyyo dakkhiṇeyyo añjalikaraṇīyo anuttaraṃ puññakkhettaṃ lokassā ti. XCVIII.

1. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato thero[^201-1] bhikkhu yassaṃ yassaṃ[^201-1] disāyaṃ viharati phāsu yeva viharati. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Thero hoti rattaññū cirapabbajito, sīlavā hoti . . .[^201-2] samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu, bahussuto hoti . . .[^201-3] diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, ubhayāni kho pan'; assa pātimokkhāni vitthārena svāgatāni honti suvibhattāni suppavattīni[^201-4] suvinicchitāni suttaso anuvyañjanaso, adhikaraṇasamuppādavūpasamakusalo hoti, dhammakāmo hoti piyasamudāhāro[^201-5] abhidhamme abhivinaye uḷārapāmujjo[^201-6], santuṭṭho hoti itarītaracīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagīlānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena, pāsādiko hoti abhikkantapaṭikkante[^201-7] susaṃvuto[^201-8] antaraghare pi[^201-9] nisajjāya, catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ abhicetasikānaṃ[^201-10] diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hoti[^201-11] akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ ca[^201-12] khayā anāsavaṃ cetovimuttiṃ paññāvimuttiṃ diṭṭh'; eva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgato thero bhikkhu yassaṃ yassaṃ[^201-13] disāyaṃ viharati phāsu yeva viharatī ti. XCIX.

1. Atha kho āyasmā Upāli yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 202 -->
<a id="page-202"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 202]*

nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Upāli Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘icchām'; ahaṃ bhante araññe[^202-1] vanapatthāni[^202-1] pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevitun'; ti.

2. Durabhisambhavāni[^202-2] kho Upāli araññe vanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni, dukkaraṃ pavivekaṃ durabhiramaṃ ekatte[^202-3], haranti[^202-4] maññe mano vanāni samādhiṃ[^202-5] alabhamānassa bhikkhuno. Yo kho Upāli evaṃ vadeyya ‘ahaṃ samādhiṃ alabhamāno araññe vanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevissāmī'; ti, tass'; etaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ: saṃsīdissati vā uppilavissati[^202-6] vā.

3. Seyyathā pi Upāli mahā-udakarahado. Atha āgaccheyya hatthināgo sattaratano vā aṭṭharatano[^202-7] vā. Tassa evam assa ‘yan nūnāhaṃ imaṃ udakarahadaṃ ogāhetvā kaṇṇasandhovikaṃ[^202-8] pi khiḍḍaṃ kiḷeyyaṃ, piṭṭhisandhovikaṃ pi khiḍḍaṃ kiḷeyyaṃ, kaṇṇasandhovikaṃ pi khiḍḍaṃ kiḷitvā[^202-9] piṭṭhisandhovikaṃ pi khiḍḍaṃ kiḷitvā nahātvā[^202-10] ca pivitvā ca paccuttaritvā yena kāmaṃ pakkameyyan'[^202-11] ti. So taṃ udakarahadaṃ ogāhetvā kaṇṇasandhovikaṃ pi khiḍḍaṃ kiḷeyya, piṭṭhisandhovikaṃ pi khiḍḍaṃ kiḷeyya, kaṇṇasandhovikaṃ pi khiḍḍaṃ kiḷitvā piṭṭhisandhovikaṃ pi khiḍḍaṃ kiḷitvā nahātvā ca pivitvā ca paccuttaritvā yena kāmaṃ pakkameyya. Taṃ kissa hetu? Mahā h'; Upāli[^202-12] attabhāvo gambhīre gādhaṃ vindati[^202-13]. Atha āgaccheyya saso[^202-14] vā biḷāro vā. Tassa evam assa ‘ko cāhaṃ ko ca hatthināgo? Yan nūnāhaṃ imaṃ udakarahadaṃ ogāhetvā kaṇṇasandhovikaṃ pi khiḍḍaṃ kiḷeyyaṃ, piṭṭhisandhovikaṃ pi khiḍḍaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 203 -->
<a id="page-203"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 203]*

kiḷeyyaṃ, kaṇṇasandhovikaṃ pi khiḍḍaṃ kiḷitvā piṭṭhisandhovikaṃ khiḍḍaṃ kiḷitvā nahātvā ca pivitvā ca paccuttaritvā yena kāmaṃ pakkameyyan'; ti. So taṃ udakarahadaṃ[^203-1] sahasā appaṭisaṃkhāya[^203-2] pakkhandeyya[^203-3]. Tass' etaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ: saṃsīdissati vā uppilavissati[^203-4] vā[^203-5]. Taṃ kissa hetu? Paritto h'; Upāli[^203-6] attabhāvo gambhīre gādhaṃ na vindati[^203-7]. Evam eva kho Upāli yo evaṃ vadeyya ‘ahaṃ samādhiṃ alabhamāno araññe vanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevissāmī'; ti, tass'; etaṃ pāṭikaṅkhaṃ: saṃsīdissati vā uppilavissati[^203-8] vā.

4. Seyyathā pi Upāli daharo kumāro[^203-9] mando uttānaseyyako sakena muttakarīsena kiḷati. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Upāli ‘nanvāyaṃ[^203-10] kevalā paripūrā bālakhiḍḍā'[^203-11] ti? Evaṃ bhante. Sa kho so Upāli kumāro aparena samayena vuddhim anvāya indriyānaṃ paripākam anvāya[^203-12], yāni tāni kumārakānaṃ kiḷāpanakāni bhavanti, seyyathīdaṃ vaṅkaṃ[^203-13] ghaṭikaṃ mokkhacikaṃ[^203-14] ciṅgulakaṃ[^203-15] pattāḷhakaṃ rathakaṃ dhanukaṃ, tehi kiḷati. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Upāli ‘nanvāyaṃ[^203-16] khiḍḍā purimāya khiḍḍāya[^203-17] abhikkantatarā ca[^203-17] paṇītatarā cā'; ti? Evaṃ bhante. Sa[^203-18] kho[^203-18] so Upāli kumāro aparena samayena vuddhim anvāya indriyānaṃ[^203-19] paripākam[^203-19] anvāya[^203-19] pañcahi kāmaguṇehi samappito samaṅgībhūto paricāreti[^203-20]: cakkhuviññeyyehi rūpehi iṭṭhehi kantehi manāpehi piyarūpehi kāmūpasaṃhitehi rajanīyehi, sotaviññeyyehi saddehi . . . ghānaviññeyyehi gandhehi . . . jivhāviññeyyehi rasehi . . . kāyaviññeyyehi


<!-- PTS Page 204 -->
<a id="page-204"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 204]*

phoṭṭhabbehi iṭṭhehi kantehi manāpehi piyarūpehi kāmūpasaṃhitehi rajanīyehi. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi[^204-1] Upāli ‘nanvāyaṃ[^204-2] khiḍḍā purimāhi khiḍḍāhi abhikkantatarā ca paṇītatarā cā'; ti? Evaṃ bhante.

5. Idha kho pana vo[^204-3] Upāli Tathāgato loke uppajjati arahaṃ sammāsambuddho vijjācaraṇasampanno sugato lokavidū anuttaro purisadammasārathi Satthā devamanussānaṃ buddho Bhagavā[^204-4]. So imaṃ lokaṃ sadevakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ {sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ}[^204-5] pajaṃ sadevamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedeti[^204-6]. So dhammaṃ deseti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhe kalyāṇaṃ pariyosānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ savyañjanaṃ kevalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāseti. Taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputto vā aññatarasmiṃ vā kule paccājāto[^204-7]. So taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā Tathāgate saddhaṃ paṭilabhati. So tena saddhāpaṭilābhena samannāgato iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘sambādho gharāvāso rajāpatho[^204-8], abbhokāso pabbajjā; na yidaṃ[^204-9] sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā ekantaparipuṇṇaṃ ekantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ; yan nūnāhaṃ kesamassuṃ ohāretvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajeyyan'; ti. So aparena samayena appaṃ vā bhogakkhandhaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā bhogakkhandhaṃ pahāya appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya kesamassuṃ ohāretvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati. So evaṃ pabbajito samāno bhikkhūnaṃ[^204-10] sikkhāsājīvasamāpanno pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti, nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati. Adinnādānaṃ[^204-11] pahāya[^204-11] adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, dinnādāyī dinnapāṭikaṅkhī athenena sucibhūtena attanā viharati. Abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī hoti,


<!-- PTS Page 205 -->
<a id="page-205"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 205]*

ārācārī[^205-1] virato methunā gāmadhammā. Musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭivirato hoti, saccavādī saccasandho theto paccayiko avisaṃvādako lokassa, pisunaṃ[^205-2] vācaṃ pahāya pisunāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti; na ito sutvā amutra akkhātā imesaṃ bhedāya, amutra vā[^205-3] sutvā na[^205-4] imesaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhedāya; iti bhinnānaṃ vā sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā samaggārāmo samaggarato samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ[^205-5] vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, yā sā vācā nelā[^205-6] kaṇṇasukhā pemaniyā hadayaṅgamā porī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā, tathārūpiṃ[^205-7] vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī[^205-8] dhammavādī[^205-3] vinayavādī[^205-3], nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti[^205-4] kālena sāpadesaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ. So bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭivirato hoti. Ekabhattiko hoti rattuparato virato vikālabhojanā. Naccagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭivirato hoti. Mālāgandhavilepanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭivirato hoti. Uccāsayanamahāsayanā[^205-9] paṭivirato hoti[^205-10]. Jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭivirato hoti. Āmakadhaññapaṭiggahaṇā paṭivirato hoti. Āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭivirato hoti. Itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā[^205-11] paṭivirato hoti. Dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā[^205-12] paṭivirato hoti. Ajeḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭivirato hoti. Kukkuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭivirato hoti. Hatthigavassavaḷavāpaṭiggahaṇā[^205-6] paṭivirato[^205-6] hoti[^205-6]. Khettavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭivirato hoti. Dūteyyapahiṇagamanānuyogā[^205-13] paṭivirato hoti. Kayavikkayā paṭivirato hoti. Tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭivirato


<!-- PTS Page 206 -->
<a id="page-206"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 206]*

hoti. Ukkoṭanavañcananikatisāciyogā[^206-1] paṭivirato hoti. Chedanavadhabandhanaviparāmosa[^206-2]-ālopasahasākārā[^206-3] paṭivirato hoti. So santuṭṭho hoti kāyaparihārikena[^206-4] cīvarena kucchiparihārikena piṇḍapātena yena yen'; eva pakkamati samādāy'; eva pakkamati. Seyyathā pi nāma pakkhī sakuṇo yena yen'; eva ḍeti sapattabhāro[^206-5] 'va[^206-6] ḍeti, evam eva bhikkhu santuṭṭho hoti kāyaparihārikena cīvarena kucchiparihārikena piṇḍapātena yena yen'; eva pakkamati samādāy'; eva pakkamati. So iminā ariyena sīlakkhandhena samannāgato ajjhattaṃ anavajjasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvedeti.

6. So cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hoti nānuvyañjanaggāhī, yatvādhikaraṇam enaṃ[^206-7] cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādomanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssaveyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipajjati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriye saṃvaraṃ āpajjati. Sotena saddaṃ sutvā . . . ghānena gandhaṃ ghāyitvā . . . jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā . . . kāyena phoṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā . . . manasā dhammaṃ viññāya na nimittaggāhī hoti nānuvyañjanaggāhī, yatvādhikaraṇam enaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjādomanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssaveyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipajjati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriye saṃvaraṃ āpajjati. So iminā ariyena indriyasaṃvarena samannāgato ajjhattaṃ avyāsekasukhaṃ[^206-8] paṭisaṃvedeti.

7. So abhikkante paṭikkante sampajānakārī hoti, ālokite vilokite sampajānakārī hoti, sammiñjite[^206-9] pasārite sampajānakārī hoti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇe sampajānakārī hoti, asite[^206-10] pīte khāyite sāyite sampajānakārī hoti, uccārapassāvakamme sampajānakārī hoti, gate ṭhite nisinne sutte jāgarite bhāsite tuṇhībhāve sampajānakārī hoti. So iminā ca ariyena sīlakkhandhena samannāgato iminā ca


<!-- PTS Page 207 -->
<a id="page-207"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 207]*

ariyena indriyasaṃvarena samannāgato iminā ca ariyena satisampajaññena samannāgato vivittaṃ senāsanaṃ bhajati araññaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ[^207-1] susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhokāsaṃ palālapuñjaṃ. So araññagato vā rukkhamūlagato vā suññāgāragato vā nisīdati pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvā[^207-2] ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya[^207-3] parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvā. so abhijjhaṃ loke pahāya vigatābhijjhena cetasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisodheti, vyāpādapadosaṃ[^207-4] pahāya avyāpannacitto[^207-4] viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, vyāpādapadosā[^207-4] cittaṃ parisodheti, thīnamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathīnamiddho viharati ālokasaññī sato sampajāno, thīnamiddhā cittaṃ parisodheti, uddhaccakukkuccaṃ pahāya anuddhato viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacitto, uddhaccakukkuccā cittaṃ parisodheti, vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikiccho viharati akathaṃkathī kusalesu dhammesu vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisodheti.

8. So[^207-5] ime pañca nīvaraṇe pahāya cetaso upakkilese paññāya dubbalīkaraṇe vivicc'; eva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ[^207-6] jhānaṃ[^207-6] upasampajja viharati. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Upāli ‘nanvāyaṃ[^207-7] vihāro purimehi[^207-8] vihārehi[^207-8] abhikkantataro ca paṇītataro cā'; ti? Evaṃ bhante. Imam pi kho Upāli mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā[^207-9] araññe vanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevanti, no ca kho tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti.

9. Puna ca paraṃ Upāli bhikkhu vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā . . .[^207-10] dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Upāli ‘nanvāyaṃ[^207-11] vihāro purimehi[^207-12] vihārehi[^207-12] abhikkantataro ca paṇītataro cā'; ti? Evaṃ bhante. Imam pi kho Upāli mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā

##### *[PTS Page 208]*

araññe vanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevanti, no ca kho tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti.

10. Puna ca paraṃ Upāli bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā . . .[^208-1] tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Upāli ‘nanvāyaṃ[^208-2] vihāro purimehi vihārehi abhikkantataro ca paṇītataro cā'; ti? Evaṃ bhante. Imam pi kho Upāli mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā araññe vanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevanti, no ca kho tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti.

11. Puna ca paraṃ Upāli bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā . . .[^208-3] catutthaṃ jhānaṃ[^208-4] upasampajja viharati. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Upāli ‘nanvāyaṃ[^208-2] vihāro purimehi vihārehi abhikkantataro ca paṇītataro cā'; ti? Evaṃ bhante. Imam pi kho mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā araññe vanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevanti, no ca kho tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti.

12. Puna ca paraṃ Upāli bhikkhu sabbaso rūpasaññānaṃ samatikkamā[^208-5] paṭighasaññānaṃ atthaṅgamā[^208-6] nānattasaññānaṃ amanasikārā ‘ananto ākāso'; ti ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Upāli ‘nanvāyaṃ[^208-2] vihāro purimehi vihārehi abhikkantataro ca paṇītataro cā' ti? Evaṃ bhante. Imam pi kho Upāli mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā araññe vanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevanti, no ca kho tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti.

13. Puna ca paraṃ Upāli bhikkhu sabbaso ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘anantaṃ viññāṇan'; ti viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ upasamapajja viharati . . . pe[^208-7] . . . sabbaso viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘natthi kiñcī'; ti ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati . . .[^208-3] sabbaso ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma ‘santam[^208-8] etaṃ paṇītam etan'

ti nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ upasampajja viharati. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Upāli ‘nanvāyaṃ[^209-1] vihāro purimehi vihārehi abhikkantataro ca paṇītataro cā'; ti? Evaṃ bhante. Imam pi kho Upāli mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā[^209-2] araññe vanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevanti, no ca kho tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti.

14. Puna ca paraṃ Upāli bhikkhu sabbaso nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samatikkamma saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ upasampajja viharati, paññāya c'; assa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā honti. Taṃ kiṃ maññasi Upāli ‘nanvāyaṃ[^209-1] vihāro purimehi vihārehi abhikkantataro ca paṇītataro cā'; ti? Evaṃ bhante. Imam pi kho Upāli mama sāvakā attani dhammaṃ sampassamānā[^209-3] araññe vanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevanti, no ca kho tāva anuppattasadatthā viharanti. Iṅgha tvaṃ Upāli saṅghe viharāhi[^209-4], saṅghe[^209-4] te[^209-4] viharato phāsu[^209-5] bhavissatī ti. C.

1. Dasa yime[^209-6] bhikkhave dhamme appahāya abhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame dasa?

2. Rāgaṃ dosaṃ mohaṃ kodhaṃ upanāhaṃ makkhaṃ paḷāsaṃ[^209-7] issaṃ macchariyaṃ mānaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave dasa dhamme appahāya abhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ[^209-8].

3. Dasa[^209-9] yime bhikkhave dhamme pahāya bhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ. Katame dasa?

4. Rāgaṃ dosaṃ mohaṃ kodhaṃ upanāhaṃ makkhaṃ paḷāsaṃ[^209-7] issaṃ[^209-10] macchariyaṃ mānaṃ. Ime kho bhikkhave dasa dhamme pahāya bhabbo arahattaṃ sacchikātun ti.

##### *[PTS Page 210]*

Upāsakavaggo[^210-1] dasamo[^210-2]. Tatr'[^210-3] uddānaṃ: Kāmabhogī[^210-4] veraṃ[^210-5] diṭṭhi[^210-6] Vajjiya[^210-7]-Uttiyā[^210-8] ubho[^210-9] Kokanudo[^210-10] āhuniyo[^210-11] thero[^210-12] Upāli abhabbo[^210-13] ti[^210-14]. Dutiyapaṇṇāsako[^210-15] niṭṭhito[^210-16]. CI.

1. Tisso[^210-17] bhikkhave samaṇasaññā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhamme paripūrenti. Katamā tisso?

2. Vevaṇṇiyamhi ajjhūpagato, parapaṭibaddhā[^210-18] me jīvikā[^210-19], añño me ākappo karaṇīyo ti[^210-20]. Imā kho bhikkhave tisso samaṇasaññā bhāvitā bahulīkatā satta dhamme paripūrenti. Katame satta?

3. Niccaṃ[^210-21] satatakārī hoti satatavuttī[^210-22] sīlesu, anabhijjhālu hoti, avyāpajjho[^210-23] hoti, anatimānī hoti, sikkhākāmo


<!-- PTS Page 211 -->
<a id="page-211"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 211]*

hoti, idam atthan ti 'ssa[^211-1] hoti jīvitaparikkhāresu, āraddhaviriyo ca[^211-2] viharati. Imā kho bhikkhave tisso samaṇasaññā bhāvitā bahulīkatā ime satta dhamme paripūrentī ti. CII.

1. Satt'; ime bhikkhave bojjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā tisso vijjā paripūrenti. Katame satta?

2. Satisambojjhaṅgo, dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo, viriyasambojjhaṅgo, pītisambojjhaṅgo, passaddhisambojjhaṅgo, samādhisambojjhaṅgo, upekhāsambojjhaṅgo[^211-3]. Ime kho bhikkhave satta bojjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā tisso vijjā paripūrenti. Katamā tisso?

3. Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati, seyyathīdaṃ ekam pi jātiṃ dve pi jātiyo[^211-4] . . . pe[^211-5] . . . iti sākāraṃ sa-uddesaṃ anekavihitaṃ pubbenivāsaṃ anussarati. Dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena[^211-6] . . . pe[^211-7] . . . yathākammūpage satte pajānāti. Āsavānaṃ khayā . . . pe[^211-8] . . . sacchikatvā upasampajja viharati. Ime kho bhikkhave satta bojjhaṅgā bhāvitā bahulīkatā imā tisso vijjā paripūrentī ti. CIII.

1. Micchattaṃ bhikkhave āgamma virādhanā hoti, no ārādhanā. Kathañ ca bhikkhave micchattaṃ āgamma virādhanā hoti, no ārādhanā?

2. Micchādiṭṭhikassa bhikkhave micchāsaṅkappo pahoti. Micchāsaṅkappassa micchāvācā pahoti. Micchāvācassa


<!-- PTS Page 212 -->
<a id="page-212"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 212]*

micchākammanto pahoti. Micchākammantassa micchā-ājīvo pahoti. Micchā-ājīvassa micchāvāyāmo pahoti. Micchāvāyāmassa micchāsati pahoti. Micchāsatissa micchāsamādhi pahoti. Micchāsamādhissa micchāñāṇaṃ pahoti. Micchāñāṇassa[^212-1] micchāvimutti pahoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave micchattaṃ āgamma virādhanā hoti, no ārādhanā.

3. Sammattaṃ bhikkhave āgamma ārādhanā hoti, no virādhanā. Kathañ ca bhikkhave sammattaṃ āgamma ārādhanā hoti, no virādhanā?

4. Sammādiṭṭhikassa bhikkhave sammāsaṅkappo pahoti. Sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācā pahoti. Sammāvācassa sammākammanto pahoti. Sammākammantassa sammā-ājīvo pahoti. Sammā-ājīvassa sammāvāyāmo pahoti. Sammāvāyāmassa sammāsati pahoti. Sammāsatissa sammāsamādhi pahoti. Sammāsamādhissa sammāñāṇaṃ pahoti. Sammāñāṇassa[^212-2] sammāvimutti pahoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave sammattaṃ āgamma ārādhanā hoti, no virādhanā ti. CIV.

1. Micchādiṭṭhikassa bhikkhave purisapuggalassa micchāsaṅkappassa micchāvācassa micchākammantassa micchāājīvassa micchāvāyāmassa micchāsatissa micchāsamādhissa micchāñāṇassa[^212-2] micchāvimuttissa yañ c'; eva[^212-3] kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhisamattaṃ samādiṇṇaṃ[^212-4] yañ ca vacīkammaṃ . . . yañ ca manokammaṃ yathādiṭṭhisamattaṃ samādiṇṇaṃ yā ca cetanā yā ca patthanā yo ca paṇidhi ye ca saṅkhārā, sabbe te dhammā aniṭṭhāya akantāya amanāpāya ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Diṭṭhi hi[^212-5] bhikkhave pāpikā.

2. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave nimbabījaṃ vā kosātakībījaṃ[^212-6] vā tittakalābubījaṃ[^212-7] vā allāya paṭhaviyā[^212-8] nikkhittaṃ, yañ


<!-- PTS Page 213 -->
<a id="page-213"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 213]*

c'; eva paṭhavirasaṃ upādiyati yañ ca āporasaṃ upādiyati, sabban taṃ tittakattāya[^213-1] kaṭukattāya asātattāya saṃvattati. Taṃ kissa hetu? Bījaṃ hi[^213-2] bhikkhave pāpakaṃ. Evam eva kho bhikkhave micchādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa micchāsaṅkappassa micchāvācassa micchākammantassa micchā-ājīvassa micchāvāyāmassa micchāsatissa micchāsamādhissa micchāñāṇassa[^213-3] micchāvimuttissa yañ c'; eva kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhisamattaṃ samādiṇṇaṃ yañ ca vacīkammaṃ . . .[^213-4] yañ[^213-5] ca manokammaṃ yathādiṭṭhisamattaṃ samādiṇṇaṃ yā ca cetanā yā ca patthanā yo ca paṇidhi ye ca saṅkhārā, sabbe te dhammā aniṭṭhāya akantāya amanāpāya ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Diṭṭhi hi[^213-6] bhikkhave pāpikā.

3. Sammādiṭṭhikassa bhikkhave purisapuggalassa sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācassa sammākammantassa sammāājīvassa sammāvāyāmassa sammāsatissa sammāsamādhissa sammāñāṇassa sammāvimuttissa yañ c'; eva kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhisamattaṃ samādiṇṇaṃ, yañ ca vacīkammaṃ yathādiṭṭhisamattaṃ samādiṇṇaṃ yañ ca manokammaṃ yathādiṭṭhisamattaṃ samādiṇṇaṃ yā ca cetanā yā ca patthanā yo ca paṇidhi ye ca saṅkhārā, sabbe te dhammā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Diṭṭhi hi[^213-6] bhikkhave bhaddikā.

4. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave ucchubījaṃ vā sālibījaṃ vā muddikabījaṃ[^213-7] vā allāya paṭhaviyā nikkhittaṃ, yañ c'; eva[^213-8] paṭhavirasaṃ upādiyati yañ ca āporasaṃ upādiyati, sabban taṃ sātattāya madhurattāya asecanakattāya saṃvattati. Taṃ kissa hetu? Bījaṃ hi bhikkhave bhaddakaṃ[^213-9]. Evam eva kho bhikkhave sammādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa[^213-10] sammāsaṅkappassa. sammāvācassa sammākammantassa


<!-- PTS Page 214 -->
<a id="page-214"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 214]*

sammā-ājīvassa sammāvāyāmassa sammāsatissa sammāsamādhissa sammāñāṇassa sammāvimuttissa yañ c'; eva kāyakammaṃ yathādiṭṭhisamattaṃ samādiṇṇaṃ yañ ca vacīkammaṃ . . . yañ ca manokammaṃ yathādiṭṭhisamattaṃ samādiṇṇaṃ yā ca cetanā yā ca patthanā yo ca paṇidhi ye ca saṅkhārā, sabbe te dhammā iṭṭhāya kantāya manāpāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Diṭṭhi hi[^214-1] bhikkhave bhaddikā ti. CV.

1. Avijjā bhikkhave pubbaṅgamā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā anvad[^214-2] eva[^214-2] ahirikaṃ anottappaṃ. Avijjāgatassa bhikkhave aviddasuno[^214-3] micchādiṭṭhi pahoti. Micchādiṭṭhikassa[^214-4] micchāsaṅkappo pahoti. Micchāsaṅkappassa micchāvācā pahoti. Micchāvācassa micchākammanto pahoti. Micchākammantassa micchā-ājīvo pahoti. Micchāājīvassa micchāvāyāmo pahoti. Micchāvāyāmassa micchāsati pahoti. Micchāsatissa micchāsamādhi pahoti. Micchāsamādhissa micchāñāṇaṃ pahoti. Micchāñāṇassa micchāvimutti pahoti.

2. Vijjā[^214-5] bhikkhave pubbaṅgamā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā anvad eva hirottappaṃ. Vijjāgatassa bhikkhave viddasuno[^214-6] sammādiṭṭhi pahoti. Sammādiṭṭhikassa[^214-4] sammāsaṅkappo pahoti. Sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācā pahoti. Sammāvācassa sammākammanto pahoti. Sammākammantassa sammā-ājīvo pahoti. Sammā-ājīvassa sammāvāyāmo pahoti. Sammāvāyāmassa sammāsati pahoti. Sammāsatissa sammāsamādhi pahoti. Sammāsamādhissa sammāñāṇaṃ pahoti. Sammāñāṇassa sammāvimutti pahoti.


<!-- PTS Page 215 -->
<a id="page-215"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 215]*

CVI.

1. Dasa yimāni bhikkhave nijjaravatthūni[^215-1]. Katamāni dasa?

2. Sammādiṭṭhikassa bhikkhave micchādiṭṭhi nijjiṇṇā hoti, ye ca micchādiṭṭhipaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, te c'; assa nijjiṇṇā honti, sammādiṭṭhipaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti. Sammāsaṅkappassa bhikkhave micchāsaṅkappo nijjiṇṇo hoti, ye ca micchāsaṅkappapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, te c'; assa nijjiṇṇā honti, sammāsaṅkappapaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti. Sammāvācassa bhikkhave micchāvācā nijjiṇṇā hoti, ye ca micchāvācāpaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, te c'; assa nijjiṇṇā honti, sammāvācāpaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti. Sammākammantassa bhikkhave micchākammanto nijjiṇṇo hoti, ye ca micchākammantapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, te c'; assa nijjiṇṇā honti, sammākammantapaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti. Sammā-ājīvassa bhikkhave micchā-ājīvo nijjiṇṇo hoti, ye ca micchā-ājīva paccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, te c'; assa nijjiṇṇā honti, sammā-ājīvapaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti. Sammāvāyāmassa bhikkhave micchāvāyāmo nijjiṇṇo hoti, ye ca micchāvāyāmapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, te c'; assa nijjiṇṇā honti, sammāvāyāmapaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti. Sammāsatissa bhikkhave micchāsati nijjiṇṇā hoti, ye ca micchāsatipaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, te c'; assa nijjiṇṇā honti, sammāsatipaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti. Sammāsamādhissa bhikkhave micchāsamādhi nijjiṇṇo hoti, ye ca micchāsamādhipaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, te c'; assa nijjiṇṇā honti, sammāsamādhipaccayā ca aneke


<!-- PTS Page 216 -->
<a id="page-216"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 216]*

kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti. Sammāñāṇassa bhikkhave micchāñāṇaṃ nijjiṇṇaṃ hoti, ye ca micchāñāṇapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, te c'; assa nijjiṇṇā honti, sammāñāṇapaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti. Sammāvimuttissa bhikkhave micchāvimutti nijjiṇṇā hoti, ye ca micchāvimuttipaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, te c'; assa nijjiṇṇā honti. Sammāvimuttipaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti. Imāni kho bhikkhave dasa nijjaravatthūnī ti. CVII.

1. Atthi bhikkhave dakkhiṇesu janapadesu dhovanaṃ[^216-1] nāma. Tattha hoti annam pi pānam pi khajjam[^216-2] pi[^216-2] bhojjam pi leyyam pi peyyam[^216-3] pi[^216-3] naccam pi gītam pi vāditam pi. Atth'; etaṃ bhikkhave dhovanaṃ[^216-4], n'; etaṃ natthī ti vadāmi. Tañ ca kho etaṃ bhikkhave dhovanaṃ hīnaṃ gammaṃ pothujjanikaṃ[^216-5] anariyaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ na nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati[^216-6]. Ahañ[^216-7] ca[^216-7] kho[^216-7] bhikkhave ariyaṃ dhovanaṃ desissāmi[^216-8], yaṃ dhovanaṃ ekantanibbidāya virāgāya nirodhāya upasamāya abhiññāya sambodhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati; yaṃ dhovanaṃ āgamma jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuccanti, jarādhammā sattā jarāya parimuccanti, maraṇadhammā sattā maraṇena parimuccanti, sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsadhammā sattā sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsehi parimuccanti. Taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Katamañ ca taṃ[^216-3] bhikkhave ariyaṃ dhovanaṃ, yaṃ[^216-9] dhovanaṃ[^216-9] ekantanibbidāya virāgāya nirodhāya upasamāya

Ime kho bhikkhave dasa niddhamaniyā dhammā ti. CXI.

1. Atha kho aññataro bhikkhu yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so[^221-2] bhikkhu Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘asekho asekho ti bhante vuccati. Kittāvatā nu[^221-3] kho[^221-3] bhante bhikkhu asekho hotī'; ti?

1. Adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabbaṃ. Katamo ca bhikkhave adhammo[^223-3], katamo ca dhammo, katamo ca anattho, katamo ca attho?

Micchāvāyāmo bhikkhave adhammo, sammāvāyāmo dhammo, ye ca micchāvāyāmapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, sammāvāyāmapaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Micchāsati bhikkhave adhammo, sammāsati dhammo, ye ca micchāsatipaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, sammāsatipaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. 1. Adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho,


<!-- PTS Page 225 -->
<a id="page-225"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 225]*

tathā paṭipajjitabban ti. Idam avoca Bhagavā, idaṃ vatvā[^225-1] Sugato[^225-2] uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi.

2. Atha kho tesaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ acirapakkantassa Bhagavato etad ahosi: Idaṃ kho no[^225-3] āvuso[^225-3] Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban' ti. Ko nu kho imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajeyyā ti? Atha kho tesaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ etad ahosi: Ayaṃ kho āyasmā Ānando Satthu c'; eva saṃvaṇṇito sambhāvito ca viññūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahoti cāyasmā[^225-4] Ānando imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajituṃ. Yan nūna mayaṃ yenāyasmā Ānando ten' upasaṅkameyyāma[^225-5], upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ etam atthaṃ puccheyyāma[^225-5]. Yathā no āyasmā Ānando vyākarissati[^225-6], tathā naṃ dhāressāmā[^225-7] ti.

3. Atha kho te bhikkhū yenāyasmā Ānando ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Ānandena saddhiṃ sammodiṃsu; sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^225-8] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho te bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ etad avocuṃ: Idaṃ kho no āvuso Ānanda Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave[^225-9] veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban'; ti. Tesaṃ no āvuso amhākaṃ acirapakkantassa Bhagavato etad ahosi: Idaṃ kho no āvuso Bhagavā

<a id="page-226"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 226]*

saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave[^226-1] veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban' ti. Ko nu kho imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajeyyā[^226-2] ti? Tesaṃ no āvuso amhākaṃ etad ahosi: Ayaṃ kho āyasmā Ānando Satthu c'; eva saṃvaṇṇito sambhāvito ca viññūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahoti cāyasmā[^226-3] Ānando imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa {uddiṭṭhassa} vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa[^226-4] vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajituṃ. Yan nūna mayaṃ yenāyasmā Ānando ten' upasaṅkameyyāma[^226-5], upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ etam atthaṃ puccheyyāma[^226-6]. Yathā no āyasmā Ānando vyākarissati[^226-7], tathā naṃ dhāressāmā[^226-8] ti. Vibhajat'[^226-9] āyasmā Ānando ti[^226-10].

4. Seyyathā pi āvuso puriso sāratthiko sāragavesī sārapariyesanaṃ caramāno mahato rukkhassa tiṭṭhato[^226-11] sāravato atikkamm'[^226-12] eva[^226-13] mūlaṃ atikkamma[^226-14] khandhaṃ sākhāpalāse[^226-15] sāraṃ pariyesitabbaṃ maññeyya, evaṃ sampadam idaṃ. Āyasmantānaṃ Satthari sammukhībhūte taṃ Bhagavantaṃ atisitvā[^226-16] amhe etam atthaṃ paṭipucchitabbaṃ maññetha[^226-17]. So h'; āvuso[^226-18] Bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passati cakkhubhūto ñāṇabhūto dhammabhūto brahmabhūto vattā pavattā atthassa ninnetā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī[^226-19] Tathāgato. So c'; eva pan'; etassa kālo ahosi, yaṃ tumhe Bhagavantaṃ yeva upasaṅkamitvā


<!-- PTS Page 227 -->
<a id="page-227"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 227]*

etam[^227-1] atthaṃ puccheyyātha[^227-2]. Yathā vo[^227-3] Bhagavā vyākareyya, tathā naṃ dhāreyyāthā[^227-4] ti.

5. Addhāvuso[^227-5] Ānanda Bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passati cakkhubhūto ñāṇabhūto dhammabhūto brahmabhūto vattā pavattā atthassa ninnetā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī Tathāgato. So c'; eva pan'; etassa[^227-6] kālo ahosi, yaṃ mayaṃ Bhagavantaṃ yeva upasaṅkamitvā etam[^227-7] atthaṃ puccheyyāma[^227-8]. Yathā no Bhagavā vyākareyya, tathā naṃ dhāreyyāma. Api cāyasmā Ānando Satthu c'; eva saṃvaṇṇito sambhāvito ca viññūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahoti cāyasmā[^227-9] Ānando imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa[^227-10] vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajituṃ. Vibhajat'; āyasmā Ānando agarukaritvā[^227-11] ti.

6. Tena h'; āvuso[^227-12] suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evam āvuso'; ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Ānandassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Ānando etad avoca: Yaṃ kho no āvuso Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban'; ti. Katamo cāvuso adhammo, katamo ca dhammo, katamo ca anattho, katamo ca attho?

7. Micchādiṭṭhi āvuso adhammo, sammādiṭṭhi dhammo, ye ca micchādiṭṭhipaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, sammādiṭṭhipaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Micchāsaṅkappo āvuso adhammo, sammāsaṅkappo dhammo . . . pe[^227-13] . . . micchāvācā āvuso adhammo, sammāvācā


<!-- PTS Page 228 -->
<a id="page-228"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 228]*

dhammo . . . micchākammanto āvuso adhammo, sammākammanto dhammo . . . micchā-ājīvo āvuso adhammo, sammā-ājīvo dhammo . . . micchāvāyāmo āvuso adhammo, sammāvāyāmo dhammo . . . micchāsamādhi āvuso adhammo, sammāsamādhi dhammo . . . micchāñāṇaṃ āvuso adhammo, sammāñāṇaṃ dhammo . . . micchāvimutti āvuso adhammo, sammāvimutti dhammo, ye ca micchāvimuttipaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, sammāvimuttipaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Yaṃ[^228-1] kho no āvuso Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho[^228-2] ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho tathā paṭipajjitabban' ti, imassa kho ahaṃ[^228-3] āvuso Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa evaṃ vitthārena atthaṃ ājānāmi[^228-4]. Ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhe āvuso Bhagavantaṃ yeva upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ puccheyyātha[^228-5]. Yathā vo[^228-6] Bhagavā vyākaroti[^228-7], tathā naṃ dhāreyyāthā ti. ‘Evam āvuso'; ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Ānandassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumoditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho te bhikkhū Bhagavantaṃ etad avocuṃ:--

8. Yaṃ kho no bhante Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo[^228-8] ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhamañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban'; ti. Tesaṃ no bhante amhākaṃ acirapakkantassa Bhagavato etad ahosi: Idaṃ kho no āvuso Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā


<!-- PTS Page 229 -->
<a id="page-229"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 229]*

vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo[^229-1] ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban'; ti. Ko nu kho imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajeyyā ti? Tesaṃ no bhante amhākaṃ etad ahosi: Ayaṃ kho āyasmā Ānando Satthu c'; eva saṃvaṇṇito sambhāvito ca viññūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahoti cāyasmā[^229-2] Ānando imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajituṃ. Yan nūna mayaṃ yenāyasmā Ānando ten'; upasaṅkameyyāma, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ etam atthaṃ puccheyyāma. Yathā no āyasmā Ānando vyākarissati, tathā naṃ dhāressāmā[^229-3] ti. Atha kho mayaṃ bhante yenāyasmā Ānando ten'; upasaṅkamimhā[^229-4], upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ etam atthaṃ apucchimhā[^229-5]. Tesaṃ no bhante āyasmatā Ānandena imehi ākārehi imehi padehi imehi vyañjanehi attho suvibhatto ti.

9. Sādhu sādhu bhikkhave, paṇḍito bhikkhave Ānando, mahāpañño bhikkhave Ānando, maṃ ce pi tumhe bhikkhave upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ puccheyyātha, aham pi c'; etaṃ[^229-6] evam eva[^229-7] vyākareyyaṃ. Yathā taṃ Ānandena vyākataṃ, eso c'; eva[^229-8] tassa[^229-8] attho, evañ ca naṃ dhāreyyāthā ti. CXVI.

1. Atha kho Ajito[^229-9] paribbājako yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi; sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^229-10] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 230 -->
<a id="page-230"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 230]*

nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Ajito[^230-1] paribbājako Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘amhākaṃ bho Gotama paṇḍito[^230-2] nāma sabrahmacārī, tena pañcamattāni cittaṭṭhānasatāni[^230-3] cintitāni[^230-4], yehi aññatitthiyā upāraddhā[^230-5] 'va[^230-6] jānanti[^230-6] upāraddh'; amhā'[^230-7] ti. Atha kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi ‘dhāretha no[^230-8] tumhe bhikkhave paṇḍitavatthūnī'; ti. ‘Etassa Bhagavā kālo, etassa Sugata kālo, yaṃ Bhagavā bhāseyya, Bhagavato sutvā bhikkhū dhāressantī'; ti. ‘Tena hi bhikkhave suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Idha bhikkhave ekacco adhammikena vādena adhammikaṃ vādaṃ abhiniggaṇhāti[^230-9] abhinippīḷeti[^230-10]. Tena ca adhammikaṃ parisaṃ rañjeti[^230-11], tena sā adhammikā parisā uccāsaddā[^230-12] mahāsaddā[^230-12] hoti[^230-13] ‘paṇḍito vata bho paṇḍito vata bho'; ti.

3. Idha pana bhikkhave ekacco adhammikena vādena dhammikaṃ vādaṃ abhiniggaṇhāti abhinippīḷeti, tena ca adhammikaṃ parisaṃ rañjeti[^230-14], tena sā adhammikā parisā uccāsaddā mahāsaddā hoti ‘paṇḍito vata bho paṇḍito vata bho'; ti.

4. Idha pana bhikkhave ekacco adhammikena vādena dhammikañ ca vādaṃ adhammikañ ca vādaṃ abhiniggaṇhāti abhinippīḷeti, tena ca adhammikaṃ parisaṃ rañjeti[^230-14], tena sā adhammikā parisā uccāsaddā mahāsaddā hoti ‘paṇḍito vata bho paṇḍito vata bho'; ti.

5. Idha[^230-15] pana bhikkhave ekacco dhammikena vādena adhammikaṃ vādaṃ abhiniggaṇhāti abhinippīḷeti, tena ca


<!-- PTS Page 231 -->
<a id="page-231"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 231]*

dhammikaṃ[^231-1] parisaṃ rañjeti[^231-2], tena sā dhammikā[^231-1] parisā uccāsaddā mahāsaddā hoti ‘paṇḍito vata bho paṇḍito vata bho'; ti[^231-3].

6. Adhammo ca[^231-4] bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabbaṃ[^231-5]. Katamo[^231-6] ca[^231-7] bhikkhave adhammo, katamo ca[^231-7] dhammo, katamo ca[^231-7] anattho, katamo ca[^231-7] attho?

7. Micchādiṭṭhi bhikkhave adhammo, sammādiṭṭhi dhammo, ye ca micchādiṭṭhipaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, sammādiṭṭhipaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Micchāsaṅkappo bhikkhave adhammo, sammāsaṅkappo dhammo . . . micchāvācā bhikkhave adhammo, sammāvācā dhammo . . . micchākammanto bhikkhave adhammo, sammākammanto dhammo . . . micchā-ājīvo bhikkhave adhammo, sammā-ājīvo dhammo . . . micchāvāyāmo bhikkhave adhammo, sammāvāyāmo dhammo . . . micchāsati bhikkhave adhammo . . . sammāsati dhammo, micchāsamādhi bhikkhave adhammo, sammāsamādhi dhammo . . . micchāñāṇaṃ bhikkhave adhammo, sammāñāṇaṃ dhammo . . . micchāvimutti bhikkhave adhammo, sammāvimutti dhammo, ye ca micchāvimuttipaccayā aneke[^231-8] pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, sammāvimuttipaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Adhammo ca[^231-9] bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ


<!-- PTS Page 232 -->
<a id="page-232"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 232]*

ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttan ti. CXVII.

1. Atha kho Saṅgāravo[^232-1] brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi; sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ[^232-2] vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Saṅgāravo brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘kin nu kho bho Gotama orimaṃ tīraṃ, kiṃ[^232-3] pārimaṃ tīran'; ti?

2. Micchādiṭṭhi kho brāhmaṇa orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammādiṭṭhi pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Micchāsaṅkappo orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāsaṅkappo pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Micchāvācā orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāvācā pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Micchākammanto orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammākammanto pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Micchā-ājīvo orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammā-ājīvo pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Micchāvāyāmo orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāvāyāmo pārimaṃ tīraṃ, micchāsati orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāsati pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Micchāsamādhi orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāsamādhi pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Micchāñāṇaṃ orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāñāṇaṃ pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Micchāvimutti orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāvimutti pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Idaṃ kho brāhmaṇa orimaṃ tīraṃ, idaṃ pārimaṃ tīran ti[^232-3]. Appakā te manussesu ye janā pāragāmino athāyaṃ itarā pajā tīram evānudhāvati. Ye ca kho samma-d-akkhāte[^232-4] dhamme dhammānuvattino te janā pāram essanti[^232-5] maccudheyyaṃ suduttaraṃ. Kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ vippahāya sukkaṃ bhāvetha paṇḍito okā anokam āgamma viveke yattha dūramaṃ. Tatrābhiratim iccheyya hitvā kāme akiñcano pariyodapeyya attānaṃ cittaklesehi[^232-6] paṇḍito.


<!-- PTS Page 233 -->
<a id="page-233"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 233]*

Yesaṃ sambodhiyaṅgesu[^233-1] sammācittaṃ subhāvitaṃ ādānapaṭinissagge anupādāya ye ratā khīṇāsavā jutimanto[^233-2] te loke parinibbutā[^233-3] ti. CXVIII.

1. Orimañ ca vo[^233-4] bhikkhave tīraṃ desissāmi[^233-5] pārimañ ca tīraṃ. Taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Katamañ ca bhikkhave orimaṃ tīraṃ, katamañ ca pārimaṃ tīraṃ? Micchādiṭṭhi bhikkhave[^233-6] orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammādiṭṭhi pārimaṃ tīraṃ . . . pe[^233-7] . . . micchāvimutti orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammāvimutti pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Idaṃ kho bhikkhave orimaṃ tīraṃ, idaṃ pārimaṃ tīran ti. Appakā te manussesu ye janā pāragāmino athāyaṃ itarā pajā tīram evānudhāvati. Ye ca kho samma-d-akkhāte dhamme[^233-8] dhammānuvattino te janā pāram essanti maccudheyyaṃ suduttaraṃ. Kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ vippahāya sukkaṃ bhāvatha paṇḍito okā anokam āgamma viveke yattha dūramaṃ. Tatrābhiratim iccheyya hitvā kāme akiñcano pariyodapeyya attānaṃ cittaklesehi paṇḍito. Yesaṃ sambodhiyaṅgesu[^233-1] sammācittaṃ subhāvitaṃ ādānapaṭinissagge anupādāya ye ratā khīṇāsavā jutimanto[^233-2] te loke parinibbutā[^233-3] ti. CXIX.

1. Tena kho pana samayena Jāṇussoṇi[^233-9] brāhmaṇo tadah' uposathe sīsaṃ nahāto navaṃ khomayugaṃ nivattho allaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 234 -->
<a id="page-234"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 234]*

kusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya Bhagavato avidūre ekamantaṃ ṭhito hoti. Addasā[^234-1] kho Bhagavā Jāṇussoṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ tadah'; uposathe sīsaṃ nahātaṃ navaṃ khomayugaṃ nivatthaṃ allaṃ kusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya avidūre ekamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ, disvā[^234-2] Jāṇussoṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ etad avoca ‘kin nu kho[^234-3] tvaṃ brāhmaṇa tadah'; uposathe sīsaṃ nahāto navaṃ khomayugaṃ nivattho allaṃ kusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya ekamantaṃ ṭhito, kin nu[^234-4] kho[^234-5] ajja[^234-5] brāhmaṇakulassā'[^234-6] ti? ‘Paccorohaṇī bho Gotama ajja brāhmaṇakulassā'[^234-6] ti. Yathākathaṃ pana brāhmaṇa brāhmaṇānaṃ paccorohaṇī hotī'; ti? ‘Idha bho Gotama brāhmaṇā tadah'; uposathe sīsaṃ nahātā navaṃ khomayugaṃ nivatthā allena gomayena paṭhaviṃ opuñjitvā[^234-7] haritehi kusehi pattharitvā[^234-8] antarā ca velaṃ antarā ca agyāgāraṃ[^234-9] seyyaṃ kappenti. Te taṃ rattiṃ tikkhattuṃ paccuṭṭhāya[^234-10] pañjalikā[^234-10] aggiṃ namassanti "paccorohāma bhavantaṃ[^234-11], paccorohāma bhavantan[^234-11]" ti, pahutena[^234-12] ca sappitelena navanītena aggiṃ santappenti, tassā ca rattiyā accayena paṇītena khādaniyena bhojaniyena brāhmaṇe santappenti. Evaṃ bho Gotama brāhmaṇānaṃ paccorohaṇī hoti'; ti. ‘Aññathā kho brāhmaṇa[^234-13] brāhmaṇānaṃ paccorohaṇī[^234-14], aññathā ca[^234-15] pana ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇī hotī'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana bho Gotama ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇī hoti[^234-16]? Sādhu me bhavaṃ Gotama tathā dhammaṃ desetu, yathā ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇī hotī'; ti[^234-17]. ‘Tena hi brāhmaṇa suṇāhi sādhukaṃ manasikarohi, bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bho'; ti kho Jāṇussoṇi brāhmaṇo Bhagavato paccassosi. Bhagavā etad avoca:--


<!-- PTS Page 235 -->
<a id="page-235"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 235]*

2. Idha brāhmaṇa ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘micchādiṭṭhiyā kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahati, micchādiṭṭhiyā paccorohati . . . ‘Micchāsaṅkappassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchāsaṅkappaṃ pajahati, micchāsaṅkappā paccorohati . . . ‘Micchāvācāya kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchāvācaṃ pajahati, micchāvācāya paccorohati . . . ‘Micchākammantassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchākammantaṃ pajahati, micchākammantā paccorohati . . . ‘Micchā-ājīvassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchā-ājīvaṃ pajahati, micchā-ājīvā paccorohati . . . ‘Micchāvāyāmassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchāvāyāmaṃ pajahati, micchāvāyāmā paccorohati . . . ‘Micchāsatiyā kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchāsatiṃ pajahati, micchāsatiyā paccorohati . . . ‘Micchāsamādhissa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchāsamādhiṃ pajahati, micchāsamādhimhā paccorohati . . . ‘Micchāñāṇassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchāñāṇaṃ pajahati, micchāñāṇā paccorohati . . . ‘Micchāvimuttiyā kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchāvimuttiṃ pajahati, micchāvimuttiyā paccorohati. Evaṃ kho brāhmaṇa ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇī hotī ti[^235-1].

3. Aññathā bho Gotama brāhmaṇānaṃ paccorohaṇī, aññathā ca[^235-2] pana ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇī hoti[^235-3], imissā ca bho Gotama ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇiyā brāhmaṇānaṃ paccorohaṇī kalaṃ nāgghati[^235-4] soḷasiṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 236 -->
<a id="page-236"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 236]*

Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama . . . pe[^236-1] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Gotama dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇagatan ti. CXX.

1. Ariyaṃ vo bhikkhave paccorohaṇiṃ desissāmi[^236-2]. Taṃ suṇātha[^236-3] . . . pe[^236-4] . . . Katamā ca bhikkhave ariyā paccorohaṇī?

2. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘micchādiṭṭhiyā kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahati, micchādiṭṭhiyā paccorohati . . . ‘Micchāsaṅkappassa kho pāpako[^236-5] vipāko[^236-5] . . . pe[^236-4] . . . micchāvācāya kho . . . micchākammantassa kho . . . micchā-ājīvassa kho . . .[^236-6] micchāvāyāmassa kho . . . micchāsatiyā kho . . . micchāsamādhissa kho . . . micchāñāṇassa kho . . . micchāvimuttiyā kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchāvimuttiṃ pajahati, micchāvimuttiyā paccorohati. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ariyā paccorohaṇī ti. CXXI.

1. Suriyassa[^236-7] bhikkhave udayato etaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ, etaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yad[^236-8] idaṃ[^236-8] aruṇaggaṃ[^236-9]. Evam eva kho bhikkhave kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ etaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ, etaṃ pubbanimittaṃ, yad idaṃ sammādiṭṭhi.

2. Sammādiṭṭhissa[^236-10] bhikkhave sammāsaṅkappo pahoti. Sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācā pahoti. Sammāvācassa sammākammanto pahoti. Sammākammantassa sammā-ājīvo pahoti. Sammā-ājīvassa sammāvāyāmo pahoti. Sammāvāyāmassa sammāsati pahoti. Sammāsatissa sammāsamādhi

pahoti. Sammāsamādhissa sammāñāṇaṃ pahoti. Sammāñāṇassa[^237-1] sammāvimutti pahotī ti[^237-2]. CXXII.
