3. Katamo ca bhikkhave anussaritabbo dhammo? Sammādiṭṭhi . . . pe[^247-3] . . . sammāvimutti. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave anussaritabbo dhammo ti. CLIV.

1. Sacchikātabbañ ca vo bhikkhave dhammaṃ desissāmi[^247-4] na sacchikātabbañ ca, taṃ suṇātha . . . pe[^247-2] . . .

2. Katamo ca bhikkhave na sacchikātabbo dhammo? Micchādiṭṭhi . . . pe[^247-3] . . . micchāvimutti. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave na sacchikātabbo dhammo.

3. Katamo ca bhikkhave sacchikātabbo dhammo? Sammādiṭṭhi . . . pe[^247-3] . . . sammāvimutti. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave sacchikātabbo dhammo ti. Ariyamaggavaggo[^247-5] pañcamo[^247-6]. Paṇṇāsako[^247-7] tatiyo. CLV.

1. Dasahi[^247-8] bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato puggalo na sevitabbo. Katamehi dasahi[^247-9]?

. . .1 paññāya na vaḍḍhati . . .pe[^249-2] . . . paññāya vaḍḍhati[^249-3] . . .1 bahuṃ apuññaṃ pasavati . . . bahuṃ puññaṃ pasavati. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Sammādiṭṭhiko[^249-4] hoti, sammāsaṅkappo hoti, sammāvāco[^249-5] hoti, sammākammanto hoti, sammā-ājīvo hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti, sammāsati hoti, sammāsamādhi hoti, sammāñāṇī[^249-6] hoti, sammāvimutti hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgato puggalo bahuṃ puññaṃ pasavatī ti. Puggalavaggo[^249-7] chaṭṭho[^249-8]. CLXVII.

1. Tena kho pana samayena Jāṇussoṇi[^249-9] brāhmaṇo tadah' uposathe sīsaṃ nahāto navaṃ khomayugaṃ nivattho allaṃ kusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya Bhagavato avidūre ekamantaṃ ṭhito hoti. Addasā[^249-10] kho Bhagavā Jāṇussoṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ tadah'; uposathe sīsaṃ nahātaṃ navaṃ khomayugaṃ nivatthaṃ allaṃ kusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya ekamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ, disvā[^249-11] Jāṇussoṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ etad avoca ‘kin nu kho[^249-12] tvaṃ brāhmaṇa tadah'; uposathe sīsaṃ nahāto navaṃ khomayugaṃ nivattho allaṃ kusamuṭṭhiṃ ādāya ekamantaṃ ṭhito, kiṃ nu khv[^249-13] ajja brāhmaṇa brāhmaṇakulassā'[^249-14] ti[^249-15]? ‘Paccorohaṇī bho Gotama ajja brāhmaṇakulassā'[^249-14]


<!-- PTS Page 250 -->
<a id="page-250"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 250]*

ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana brāhmaṇa brāhmaṇānaṃ paccorohaṇī hotī'; ti? ‘Idha bho Gotama brāhmaṇā tadah'; uposathe sīsaṃ nahātā navaṃ khomayugaṃ nivatthā allena gomayena paṭhaviṃ opuñjitvā[^250-1] haritehi kusehi pattharitvā[^250-2] antarā ca velaṃ antarā ca agyāgāraṃ[^250-3] seyyaṃ kappenti. Te taṃ rattiṃ tikkhattuṃ paccuṭṭhāya pañjalikā aggiṃ namassanti "paccorohāma bhavantaṃ paccorohāma bhavantan" ti, pahutena[^250-4] ca sappitelena navanītena aggiṃ santappenti. Tassā ca rattiyā accayena paṇītena khādaniyena bhojaniyena brāhmaṇe santappenti. Evaṃ kho[^250-5] bho Gotama brāhmaṇānaṃ paccorohaṇī hotī'; ti. ‘Aññathā kho brāhmaṇānaṃ paccorohaṇī[^250-6], aññathā ca[^250-7] pana ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇī hotī'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana bho Gotama ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇī hoti[^250-8]? Sādhu[^250-9] me bhavaṃ Gotamo tathā dhammaṃ desetu, yathā ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇī hotī'; ti. ‘Tena hi brāhmaṇa suṇāhi sādhukaṃ manasikarohi, bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bho'; ti kho Jāṇussoṇi brāhmaṇo Bhagavato paccassosi. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Idha brāhmaṇa ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘pāṇātipātassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva[^250-10] dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā[^250-7] ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya pāṇātipātaṃ pajahati, pāṇātipātā paccorohati . . . ‘Adinnādānassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā' ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya adinnādānaṃ pajahati, adinnādānā paccorohati . . . ‘Kāmesu micchācārassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya kāmesu micchācāraṃ pajahati, kāmesu micchācārā paccorohati . . . ‘Musāvādassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti


<!-- PTS Page 251 -->
<a id="page-251"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 251]*

paṭisaṃkhāya musāvādaṃ pajahati, musāvādā paccorohati . . . ‘Pisunāya[^251-1] vācāya kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya pisunavācaṃ pajahati, pisunāya vācāya paccorohati . . . ‘Pharusāya vācāya kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya pharusavācaṃ pajahati, pharusāya vācāya paccorohati . . . ‘Samphappalāpassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya samphappalāpaṃ pajahati, samphappalāpā paccorohati . . . ‘Abhijjhāya kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā' ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya abhijjhaṃ pajahati, abhijjhāya paccorohati . . . ‘Vyāpādassa[^251-2] kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya vyāpādaṃ pajahati, vyāpādā paccorohati . . . ‘Micchādiṭṭhiyā kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahati, micchādiṭṭhiyā paccorohati. Evaṃ kho brāhmaṇa ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇī hotī ti.

3. Aññathā[^251-3] bho Gotama brāhmaṇānaṃ paccorohaṇī[^251-4] aññathā ca pana ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇī hoti, imissā ca[^251-5] bho[^251-6] Gotama ariyassa vinaye paccorohaṇiyā brāhmaṇānaṃ paccorohaṇī kalaṃ nāgghati[^251-7] soḷasiṃ. Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama . . . pe[^251-8] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan ti. CLXVIII.

1. Ariyaṃ vo bhikkhave paccorohaṇiṃ desissāmi[^251-9], taṃ suṇātha . . . pe[^251-10] . . . Bhagavā etad avoca: Katamā ca bhikkhave ariyā paccorohaṇī?


<!-- PTS Page 252 -->
<a id="page-252"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 252]*

2. Idha bhikkhave ariyasāvako iti paṭisañcikkhati ‘pāṇātipātassa kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva[^252-1] dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya pāṇātipātaṃ pajahati, pāṇātipātā paccorohati . . . pe[^252-2] . . . ‘Micchādiṭṭhiyā kho pāpako vipāko diṭṭhe c'; eva dhamme abhisamparāyañ cā'; ti. So iti paṭisaṃkhāya micchādiṭṭhiṃ pajahati, micchādiṭṭhiyā paccorohati. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave ariyā paccorohaṇī ti. CLXIX.

1. Atha kho Saṅgāravo[^252-3] brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddiṃ sammodi . . . pe[^252-4] . . . Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Saṅgāravo brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘kin nu kho bho Gotama orimaṃ tīraṃ, kiṃ pārimaṃ tīran'; ti?

2. Pāṇātipāto kho brāhmaṇa orimaṃ tīraṃ, pāṇātipātā veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Adinnādānaṃ kho[^252-5] brāhmaṇā[^252-5] orimaṃ tīraṃ, adinnādānā veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Kāmesu micchācāro orimaṃ tīraṃ, kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Musāvādo orimaṃ tīraṃ, musāvādā veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Pisunā vācā orimaṃ tīraṃ, pisunāya vācāya veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Pharusā vācā orimaṃ tīraṃ, pharusāya vācāya veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Samphappalāpo orimaṃ tīraṃ, samphappalāpā veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Abhijjhā orimaṃ tīraṃ, anabhijjhāya pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Vyāpādo orimaṃ tīraṃ, avyāpādo pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Micchādiṭṭhi orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammādiṭṭhi pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Idaṃ kho brāhmaṇa orimaṃ tīraṃ, idaṃ pārimaṃ tīran ti.


<!-- PTS Page 253 -->
<a id="page-253"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 253]*

Appakā te manussesu ye janā pāragāmino athāyaṃ[^253-1] itarā pajā tīram evānudhāvati. Ye ca kho samma-d-akkhāte dhamme dhammānuvattino te janā pāram essanti[^253-2] maccudheyyaṃ suduttaraṃ. Kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ vippahāya sukkaṃ bhāvetha paṇḍito okā anokam āgamma viveke yattha dūramaṃ. Tatrābhiratim iccheyya hitvā kāme akiñcano pariyodapeyya attānaṃ cittaklesehi[^253-3] paṇḍito. Yesaṃ sambodhiyaṅgesu[^253-4] sammācittaṃ subhāvitaṃ ādānapaṭinissagge anupādāya ye ratā khīṇāsavā jutimanto[^253-5] te loke parinibbutā[^253-6] ti[^253-7]. CLXX.

1. Orimañ ca vo[^253-8] bhikkhave tīraṃ desissāmi pārimañ ca tīraṃ, taṃ suṇātha . . . pe[^253-9] . . . Katamañ ca bhikkhave orimaṃ tīraṃ, katamañ ca pārimaṃ tīraṃ?

2. Pāṇātipāto kho bhikkhave orimaṃ tīraṃ, pāṇātipātā veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Adinnādānaṃ orimaṃ tīraṃ, adinnādānā veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Kāmesu micchācāro orimaṃ tīraṃ, kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Musāvādo orimaṃ tīraṃ, musāvādā veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Pisunā vācā orimaṃ tīraṃ, pisunāya vācāya veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Pharusā vācā orimaṃ tīraṃ, pharusāya vācāya veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Samphappalāpo orimaṃ tīraṃ, samphappalāpā veramaṇī pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Abhijjhā orimaṃ tīraṃ, anabhijjhā pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Vyāpādo orimaṃ tīraṃ, avyāpādo pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Micchādiṭṭhi orimaṃ tīraṃ, sammādiṭṭhi pārimaṃ tīraṃ. Idaṃ kho bhikkhave orimaṃ tīraṃ, idaṃ pārimaṃ tīran ti.


<!-- PTS Page 254 -->
<a id="page-254"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 254]*

Appakā te manussesu ye janā pāragāmino athāyaṃ itarā pajā tīram evānudhāvati. Ye ca kho samma-d-akkhāte dhamme dhammānuvattino te janā pāram essanti[^254-1] maccudheyyaṃ suduttaraṃ. Kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ vippahāya sukkaṃ bhāvetha paṇḍito okā anokam āgamma viveke yattha dūramaṃ. Tatrābhiratim iccheyya hitvā kāme akiñcano pariyodapeyya attānaṃ cittaklesehi paṇḍito. Yesaṃ sambodhiyaṅgesu[^254-2] sammācittaṃ subhāvitaṃ anādānapaṭinissagge anupādāya ye ratā khīṇāsavā jutimanto[^254-3] te loke parinibbutā[^254-4] ti. CLXXI.

1. Adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo anattho ca, dhammo ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā anatthañ ca, dhammañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabbaṃ[^254-5]. Katamo ca bhikkhave adhammo ca anattho ca?

2. Pāṇātipāto adinnādānaṃ kāmesu micchācāro musāvādo pisunā vācā pharusā vācā samphappalāpo abhijjhā vyāpādo micchādiṭṭhi. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave adhammo ca anattho ca. Katamo ca bhikkhave dhammo ca attho ca?

3. Pāṇātipātā veramaṇī adinnādānā veramaṇī kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇī musāvādā veramaṇī pisunāya vācāya veramaṇī pharusāya vācāya veramaṇī samphappalāpā veramaṇī anabhijjhā avyāpādo sammādiṭṭhi. Ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave dhammo ca attho ca. Adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo anattho ca, dhammo ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā anatthañ ca, dhammañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho[^254-6], tathā paṭipajjitabban ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idaṃ etaṃ paṭicca vuttan ti.


<!-- PTS Page 255 -->
<a id="page-255"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 255]*

CLXXII.

1. Adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban ti. Idam avoca Bhagavā, idaṃ vatvā[^255-1] Sugato uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāram pāvisi.

2. Atha kho tesaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ acirapakkantassa Bhagavato etad ahosi: Idaṃ kho no[^255-2] āvuso Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabho dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban' ti. Ko nu kho imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajeyyā ti? Atha kho tesaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ etad ahosi: Ayaṃ kho āyasmā Mahākaccāno Satthu c'; eva saṃvaṇṇito sambhāvito ca viññūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahoti cāyasmā[^255-3] Mahākaccāno imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajituṃ. Yan nūna mayaṃ yenāyasmā Mahākaccāno ten'; upasaṅkameyyāma, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Mahākaccānaṃ etam atthaṃ puccheyyāma. Yathā no āyasmā Mahākaccāno vyākarissati, tathā naṃ dhāressāmā[^255-4] ti.

3. Atha kho te bhikkhū yenāyasmā Mahākaccāno ten' upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Mahākaccānena saddhiṃ sammodiṃsu; sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho te bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ Mahākaccānaṃ etad avocuṃ: Idaṃ kho no āvuso Kaccāna Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañḥ


<!-- PTS Page 256 -->
<a id="page-256"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 256]*

ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban'; ti. Tesaṃ no āvuso amhākaṃ acirapakkantassa Bhagavato etad ahosi: Idaṃ kho no āvuso Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo[^256-1] dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban'; ti. Ko nu kho imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajeyyā ti? Tesaṃ no āvuso amhākaṃ etad ahosi: Ayaṃ kho āyasmā Mahākaccāno Satthu c'; eva saṃvaṇṇito sambhāvito ca viññūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahoti cāyasmā[^256-2] Mahākaccāno imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajituṃ. Yan nūna mayaṃ yenāyasmā Mahākaccāno ten' upasaṅkameyyāma, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Mahākaccānaṃ etam atthaṃ puccheyyāma. Yathā no āyasmā Mahākaccāno vyākarissati, tathā naṃ dhāressāmā ti. Vibhajat'[^256-3] āyasmā Mahākaccāno ti.

4. Seyyathā pi āvuso puriso sāratthiko sāragavesī sārapariyesanaṃ caramāno mahato rukkhassa tiṭṭhato sāravato[^256-4] atikkamm'; eva[^256-5] mūlaṃ atikkamma khandhaṃ sākhāpalāse sāraṃ pariyesitabbaṃ maññeyya, evaṃ sampadam idaṃ. Āyasmantānaṃ Satthari sammukhībhūte taṃ Bhagavantaṃ atisitvā[^256-6] amhe etam atthaṃ paṭipucchitabbaṃ maññetha[^256-7]. So h'[^256-8] āvuso Bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passati cakkhubhūto ñāṇabhūto dhammabhūto brahmabhūto vattā pavattā atthassa ninnetā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī9 Tathāgato. So c'; eva pan'; etassa kālo ahosi, yaṃ tumhe


<!-- PTS Page 257 -->
<a id="page-257"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 257]*

Bhagayantaṃ yeva upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ puccheyyātha. Yathā vo[^257-10] Bhagavā vyākareyya, tathā naṃ dhāreyyāthā[^257-2] ti.

5. Addhāvuso Kaccāna Bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti passaṃ passati cakkhubhūto ñāṇabhūto dhammabhūto brahmabhūto vattā pavattā atthassa ninnetā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī Tathāgato. So c'; eva pan'; etassa kālo ahosi, yaṃ mayaṃ Bhagavantaṃ yeva upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ puccheyyāma. Yathā no Bhagavā vyākareyya, tathā naṃ dhāreyyāma. Api cāyasmā Mahākaccāno Satthu c'; eva saṃvaṇṇito sambhāvito ca viññūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahoti cāyasmā[^257-3] Mahākaccāno imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajituṃ. Vibhajat'; āyasmā Mahākaccāno agarukaritvā[^257-4] ti.

6. Tena[^257-5] h'; āvuso[^257-5] suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evam āvuso'; ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Mahākaccānassa paccassosuṃ. Āyasmā Mahākaccāno etad avoca: Yaṃ kho no āvuso Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo[^257-6] ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban'; ti. Katamo cāvuso adhammo, katamo ca[^257-7] dhammo, katamo ca[^257-8] anattho, katamo ca[^257-9] attho?

7. Pāṇātipāto āvuso adhammo, pāṇātipātā veramaṇī dhammo, ye ca pāṇātipātapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, pāṇātipātā veramaṇīpaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Adinnādānaṃ āvuso adhammo, adinnādānā veramaṇī dhammo, ye ca adinnādānapaccayā


<!-- PTS Page 258 -->
<a id="page-258"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 258]*

aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, adinnādānā veramaṇīpaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Kāmesu micchācāro āvuso adhammo, kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇī dhammo, ye ca kāmesu micchācārapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇīpaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Musāvādo āvuso adhammo, musāvādā veramaṇī dhammo, ye ca musāvādapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, musāvādā veramaṇīpaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Pisunā vācā āvuso adhammo, pisunāya vācāya veramaṇī dhammo, ye ca pisunavācāpaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, pisunāya vācāya veramaṇīpaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Pharusā vācā āvuso adhammo, pharusāya vācāya veramaṇī dhammo, ye ca pharusavācāpaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, pharusāya vācāya veramaṇīpaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Samphappalāpo āvuso adhammo, samphappalāpā veramaṇī dhammo, ye ca samphappalāpapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, samphappalāpā veramaṇīpaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Abhijjhā āvuso adhammo, anabhijjhā dhammo, ye ca abhijjhāpaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, anabhijjhāpaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Vyāpādo āvuso adhammo, avyāpādo dhammo, ye ca vyāpādapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, avyāpādapaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Micchādiṭṭhi āvuso adhammo, sammādiṭṭhi dhammo, ye ca micchādiṭṭhipaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, sammādiṭṭhipaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Yaṃ kho no āvuso Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena


<!-- PTS Page 259 -->
<a id="page-259"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 259]*

atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho[^259-1] ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban'; ti, imassa[^259-2] kho ahaṃ āvuso Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā evaṃ vitthārena atthaṃ ājānāmi[^259-3], ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhe āvuso Bhagavantaṃ yeva upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ puccheyyātha[^259-4]. Yathā vo[^259-5] Bhagavā vyākaroti[^259-6], tathā naṃ dhāreyyāthā ti. ‘Evam āvuso'; ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato Mahākaccānassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumoditvā[^259-7] uṭṭhāyāsanā yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu. Ekamantaṃ nisinnā kho te bhikkhū Bhagavantaṃ etad avocuṃ:--

8. Yaṃ kho no[^259-8] bhante Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā utthāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho[^259-1] ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban'; ti. Tesaṃ no bhante amhākaṃ acirapakkantassa Bhagavato etad ahosi: Idaṃ kho no āvuso Bhagavā saṃkhittena uddesaṃ uddisitvā vitthārena atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭho ‘adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho[^259-1] ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban'; ti. Ko nu kho imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajeyyā ti? Tesaṃ no bhante amhākaṃ etad ahosi: Ayaṃ kho āyasmā Mahākaccāno Satthu c'; eva saṃvaṇṇito sambhāvito ca viññūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahoti cāyasmā[^259-9] Mahākaccāno imassa Bhagavatā saṃkhittena uddesassa


<!-- PTS Page 260 -->
<a id="page-260"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 260]*

uddiṭṭhassa vitthārena atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthārena atthaṃ vibhajituṃ. Yan nūna mayaṃ yenāyasmā Mahākaccāno ten'; upasaṅkameyyāma, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Mahākaccānaṃ etam atthaṃ puccheyyāma[^260-1]. Yathā no āyasmā Mahākaccāno vyākarissati, tathā naṃ dhāressāmā ti. Atha kho mayaṃ bhante yenāyasmā Mahākaccāno ten' upasaṅkamimhā, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Mahākaccānaṃ etam atthaṃ pucchimhā[^260-2]. Tesaṃ no bhante āyasmatā Mahākaccānena imehi ākārehi imehi padehi imehi vyañjanehi attho suvibhatto[^260-3] ti.

9. Sādhu sādhu bhikkhave, paṇḍito bhikkhave Mahākaccāno, mahāpañño bhikkhave Mahākaccāno, maṃ ce pi tumhe bhikkhave upasaṅkamitvā etam atthaṃ puccheyyātha[^260-1], aham pi c'; etaṃ[^260-4] evam eva[^260-5] vyākareyyaṃ[^260-6]. Yathā taṃ[^260-7] Mahākaccānena vyākataṃ, eso[^260-8] c'; eva tassa attho, evañ ca naṃ[^260-9] dhāreyyāthā ti. CLXXIII.

1. Adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban ti. Katamo ca bhikkhave adhammo, katamo ca[^260-10] dhammo, katamo ca[^260-10] anattho, katamo ca attho?

2. Pāṇātipāto bhikkhave adhammo, pāṇātipātā veramaṇī dhammo, ye ca pāṇātipātapaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, pāṇātipātā veramaṇīpaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho. Adinnādānaṃ bhikkhave adhammo, adinnādānā veramaṇī dhammo . . .[^260-11] kāmesu micchācāro


<!-- PTS Page 261 -->
<a id="page-261"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 261]*

bhikkhave adhammo, kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇī dhammo . . . musāvādo bhikkhave adhammo, musāvādā veramaṇī dhammo . . .[^261-1] pisunā vācā bhikkhave adhammo, pisunāya vācāya veramaṇī dhammo . . . pharusā vācā bhikkhave adhammo, pharusāya vācāya veramaṇī dhammo . . . samphappalāpo bhikkhave adhammo[^261-2], samphappalāpā veramaṇī dhammo . . . abhijjhā bhikkhave adhammo, anabhijjhā dhammo . . . vyāpādo bhikkhave adhammo, avyāpādo dhammo . . . micchādiṭṭhi bhikkhave adhammo, sammādiṭṭhi dhammo, ye ca micchādiṭṭhipaccayā aneke pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti, ayaṃ anattho, sammādiṭṭhipaccayā ca aneke kusalā dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, ayaṃ attho.

3. Adhammo ca bhikkhave veditabbo dhammo ca, anattho ca veditabbo attho ca, adhammañ ca viditvā dhammañ ca, anatthañ ca viditvā atthañ ca, yathā dhammo yathā attho, tathā paṭipajjitabban ti iti yan taṃ vuttaṃ, idam etaṃ paṭicca vuttan ti. CLXXIV.

1. Pāṇātipātaṃ p'; ahaṃ[^261-3] bhikkhave tividhaṃ vadāmi: lobhahetukam pi dosahetukam pi mohahetukam pi. Adinnādānaṃ p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave tividhaṃ vadāmi: lobhahetukam pi dosahetukam pi mohahetukam pi. Kāmesu micchācāraṃ p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave tividhaṃ vadāmi: lobhahetukam pi dosahetukam pi mohahetukam pi. Musāvādaṃ p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave tividhaṃ vadāmi: lobhahetukam pi dosahetukam pi mohahetukam pi. Pisunavācaṃ p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave tividhaṃ vadāmi: lobhahetukam pi dosahetukam pi mohahetukam pi. Pharusavācaṃ p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave tividhaṃ vadāmi: lobhahetukam pi dosahetukam pi mohahetukam pi. Samphappalāpaṃ p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave tividhaṃ vadāmi: lobhahetukam pi dosahetukam pi mohahetukam pi. Abhijjhaṃ[^261-4]


<!-- PTS Page 262 -->
<a id="page-262"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 262]*

p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave tividhaṃ vadāmi: lobhahetukam pi dosahetukam pi mohahetukam pi. Vyāpādaṃ[^262-1] p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave tividhaṃ vadāmi: lobhahetukam pi dosahetukam pi mohahetukam pi. Micchādiṭṭhiṃ[^262-1] p'; ahaṃ bhikkhave tividhaṃ vadāmi: lobhahetukam pi dosahetukam pi mohahetukam pi.

2. Iti kho bhikkhave lobho kammanidānasambhavo doso kammanidānasambhavo moho kammanidānasambhavo, lobhakkhayā[^262-2] kammanidānasaṃkhayo dosakkhayā kammanidānasaṃkhayo mohakkhayā kammanidānasaṃkhayo ti. CLXXV.

1. Saparikkamano ayaṃ bhikkhave dhammo nāyaṃ dhammo aparikkamano. Kathañ ca bhikkhave saparikkamano ayaṃ dhammo nāyaṃ dhammo aparikkamano?

2. Pāṇātipātissa bhikkhave pāṇātipātā veramaṇī parikkamanaṃ hoti, adinnādāyissa bhikkhave adinnādānā veramaṇī parikkamanaṃ hoti, kāmesu micchācārassa[^262-3] bhikkhave kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇī parikkamanaṃ hoti, musāvādassa[^262-4] bhikkhave musāvādā veramaṇī parikkamanaṃ hoti, pisunāvācassa bhikkhave pisunāya[^262-5] vācāya[^262-5] veramaṇī parikkamanaṃ hoti, pharusāvācassa bhikkhave pharusāya vācāya[^262-6] veramaṇī parikkamanaṃ hoti, samphappalāpassa[^262-7] bhikkhave samphappalāpā veramaṇī parikkamanaṃ hoti, abhijjālussa bhikkhave anabhijjhā parikkamanaṃ hoti, vyāpādassa[^262-8] bhikkhave avyāpādo parikkamanaṃ hoti, micchādiṭṭhikassa[^262-9] bhikkhave sammādiṭṭhi parikkamanaṃ hoti. Evaṃ[^262-10] kho bhikkhave parikkamanaṃ[^262-11] hoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave saparikkamano ayaṃ[^262-12] dhammo[^262-12] nāyaṃ[^262-13] dhammo[^262-13] aparikkamano ti.


<!-- PTS Page 263 -->
<a id="page-263"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 263]*

CLXXVI.

1. Ekaṃ[^263-1] samayaṃ Bhagavā Pāvāyaṃ[^263-2] viharati Cundassa kammāraputtassa ambavane. Atha kho Cundo kammāraputto yena Bhagavā ten'; upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinnaṃ kho Cundaṃ kammāraputtaṃ Bhagavā etad avoca ‘kassa no tvaṃ Cunda soceyyāni rocesī' ti? ‘Brāhmaṇā bhante pacchābhūmakā kamaṇḍalukā[^263-3] sevālamālakā[^263-4] aggiparicārikā udakorohakā soceyyāni paññāpenti, tesāhaṃ soceyyāni[^263-5] rocemī'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana Cunda brāhmaṇā pacchābhūmakā kamaṇḍalukā sevālamālakā aggiparicārikā udakorohakā soceyyāni paññāpentī'; ti? ‘Idha bhante brāhmaṇā pacchābhūmakā kamaṇḍalukā[^263-3] sevālamālakā aggiparicārikā udakorohakā, te sāvakaṃ[^263-6] evaṃ samādapenti ‘ehi tvaṃ ambho purisa kālass'; eva[^263-7] vuṭṭhahanto[^263-8] 'va[^263-9] sayanamhā paṭhaviṃ āmaseyyāsi; no ce paṭhaviṃ āmaseyyāsi, allāni[^263-10] gomayāni[^263-10] āmaseyyāsi; no ce allāni[^263-11] gomayāni[^263-11] āmaseyyāsi, haritāni tiṇāni āmaseyyāsi; no ce haritāni tiṇāni āmaseyyāsi, aggiṃ paricareyyāsi; no ce aggiṃ paricareyyāsi, pañjaliko ādiccaṃ namasseyyāsi, no ce pañjaliko ādiccaṃ namasseyyāsi, sāyatatiyakaṃ udakaṃ oroheyyāsī'[^263-12] ti. ‘Evaṃ kho[^263-13] bhante brāhmaṇā pacchābhūmakā kamaṇḍalukā sevālamālakā aggiparicārikā udakorohakā soceyyāni paññāpenti, tesāhaṃ soceyyāni rocemī'; ti. ‘Aññathā kho Cunda brāhmaṇā pacchābhūmakā kamaṇḍalukā sevālamālakā aggiparicārikā udakorohakā soceyyāni paññāpenti, aññathā ca


<!-- PTS Page 264 -->
<a id="page-264"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 264]*

pana ariyassa vinaye soceyyaṃ hotī'; ti. ‘Yathākathaṃ pana bhante ariyassa vinaye soceyyaṃ hoti? Sādhu me bhante Bhagavā tathā dhammaṃ desetu, yathā ariyassa vinaye soceyyaṃ hotī'; ti. ‘Tena hi Cunda suṇāhi sādhukaṃ manasikarohi, bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho Cundo kammāraputto Bhagavato paccassosi. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Tividhaṃ kho Cunda kāyena asoceyyaṃ hoti, catubbidhaṃ vācāya asoceyyaṃ hoti, tividhaṃ manasā asoceyyaṃ hoti. Kathañ ca Cunda tividhaṃ kāyena asoceyyaṃ hoti?

3. Idha Cunda ekacco pāṇātipātī hoti luddo[^264-1] lohitapāṇī hatapahate[^264-2] niviṭṭho adayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtesu[^264-3]. Adinnādāyī hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ gāmagataṃ vā araññagataṃ vā, taṃ[^264-4] adinnaṃ theyyasaṃkhātaṃ ādātā[^264-5] hoti. Kāmesu micchācārī[^264-6] hoti, yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā[^264-7] bhāturakkhitā[^264-8] bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā[^264-9] dhammarakkhitā[^264-10] sassāmikā[^264-11] saparidaṇḍā antamaso mālāguṇaparikkhittā[^264-12] pi[^264-13], tathārūpāsu cārittaṃ āpajjitā hoti. Evaṃ kho Cunda tividhaṃ kāyena asoceyyaṃ hoti. Kathañ ca Cunda catubbidhaṃ vācāya asoceyyaṃ hoti?

4. Idha Cunda ekacco musāvādī hoti, sabhāgato[^264-14] vā parisagato[^264-15] vā {ñātimajjhagato} vā pūgamajjhagato vā rājakulamajjhagato vā abhinīto sakkhipuṭṭho[^264-16] ‘eh'; ambho[^264-17] purisa, yaṃ jānāsi, taṃ vadehī'; ti[^264-18] so ajānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^264-19] jānāmī'; ti jānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^264-19] na jānāmī'; ti[^264-20] apassaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^264-19] passāmī'; ti passaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^264-19] na passāmī'; ti iti


<!-- PTS Page 265 -->
<a id="page-265"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 265]*

attahetu vā parahetu vā āmisakiñcikkhahetu vā sampajānamusā bhāsitā hoti. Pisunāvāco hoti, ito sutvā amutra akkhātā imesaṃ bhedāya amutra vā[^265-1] sutvā imesaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhedāya iti samaggānaṃ vā bhettā[^265-2] bhinnānaṃ vā anuppadātā vaggārāmo vaggarato vagganandī vaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Pharusāvāco hoti, yā sā vācā aṇḍakā[^265-3] kakkasā[^265-4] parakaṭukā parābhisajjanī kodhasāmantā asamādhisaṃvattanikā, tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Samphappalāpī hoti, akālavādī abhūtavādī anatthavādī adhammavādī[^265-5] avinayavādī anidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti[^265-6] akālena anapadesaṃ apariyantavatiṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ. Evaṃ kho Cunda catubbidhaṃ vācāya asoceyyaṃ hoti. Kathañ ca Cunda tividhaṃ manasā asoceyyaṃ hoti?

5. Idha Cunda ekacco abhijjhālu hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ, taṃ abhijjhitā[^265-7] hoti ‘aho vata[^265-8] yaṃ[^265-8] parassa, taṃ mama[^265-9] assā'; ti. Vyāpannacitto hoti paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappo ‘ime sattā haññantu vā bajjhantu[^265-10] vā ucchijjantu[^265-11] vā vinassantu vā mā vā ahesuṃ iti[^265-12] vā'[^265-12] ti. Micchādiṭṭhiko hoti viparītadassano[^265-13] ‘natthi dinnaṃ natthi yiṭṭhaṃ natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, natthi ayaṃ loko natthi paro[^265-14] loko[^265-14], natthi mātā natthi pitā natthi sattā opapātikā, natthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā[^265-15] sammāpaṭipannā[^265-16], ye imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti. Evaṃ kho Cunda tividhaṃ manasā asoceyyaṃ hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 266 -->
<a id="page-266"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 266]*

6. Ime kho Cunda dasa akusalakammapathā. Imehi kho Cunda dasahi akusalehi kammapathehi samannāgato kālass' eva[^266-1] vuṭṭhahanto[^266-2] 'va sayanamhā paṭhaviṃ ce pi āmasati, asuci yeva hoti. No ce pi paṭhaviṃ āmasati, asuci yeva hoti. Allāni ce pi gomayāni āmasati, asuci yeva hoti. No ce pi allāni gomayāni āmasati, asuci yeva hoti. Haritāni ce pi tiṇāni āmasati, asuci yeva hoti. No ce pi haritāni tiṇāni āmasati, asuci yeva hoti. Aggiṃ ce pi paricarati, asuci yeva hoti. No ce pi aggiṃ paricarati, asuci yeva hoti. Pañjaliko ce pi ādiccaṃ namassati, asuci yeva hoti. No ce pi pañjaliko ādiccaṃ namassati, asuci yeva hoti. Sāyatatiyakaṃ ce pi udakaṃ orohati, asuci yeva hoti. No ce pi sāyatatiyakaṃ udakaṃ orohati, asuci yeva hoti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Ime Cunda dasa akusalakammapathā asuci yeva honti[^266-3] asucikaraṇā ca. Imesaṃ pana Cunda dasannaṃ akusalānaṃ kammapathānaṃ sammannāgamanahetu nirayo paññāyati tiracchānayoni paññāyati pettivisayo[^266-4] paññāyati yā vā[^266-5] pan'; aññā[^266-6] pi kāci duggatiyo[^266-7].

7. Tividhaṃ kho Cunda kāyena soceyyaṃ hoti, catubbidhaṃ vācāya soceyyaṃ hoti, tividhaṃ manasā soceyyaṃ hoti. Kathañ ca Cunda tividhaṃ kāyena soceyyaṃ hoti?

8. Idha Cunda ekacco pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānupampī viharati. Adinnādānaṃ pahāya adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ gāmagataṃ vā araññagataṃ vā, na[^266-8] taṃ adinnaṃ[^266-9] theyyasaṃkhātaṃ ādātā[^266-10] hoti. Kāmesu micchācāraṃ pahāya kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti, yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā[^266-11] bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā[^266-12] dhammarakkhitā[^266-6] sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā


<!-- PTS Page 267 -->
<a id="page-267"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 267]*

antamaso mālāguṇaparikkhittā[^267-1] pi, tathārūpāsu na cārittaṃ āpajjitā hoti. Evaṃ kho Cunda tividhaṃ kāyena soceyyaṃ hoti. Kathañ ca Cunda catubbidhaṃ vācāya soceyyaṃ hoti?

9. Idha Cunda ekacco musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭivirato hoti sabhāgato vā parisagato vā ñātimajjhagato vā pūgamajjhagato vā rājakulamajjhagato vā abhinīto sakkhipuṭṭho[^267-2] ‘eh'; ambho[^267-3] purisa yaṃ jānāsi, taṃ vadehī' ti so ajānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^267-4] na jānāmī'; ti jānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^267-4] jānāmī'; ti apassaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^267-4] na passāmī'; ti passaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^267-4] passāmī'; ti iti attahetu vā parahetu vā āmisakiñcikkhahetu vā na sampajānamusā bhāsitā hoti. Pisunavācaṃ pahāya pisunāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, na ito sutvā amutra akkhātā imesaṃ bhedāya, amutra vā sutvā na imesaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhedāya, iti bhinnānaṃ vā sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā samaggārāmo samaggarato samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Pharusavācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, yā sā vācā nelā kaṇṇasukhā pemaniyā hadayaṅgamā porī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā, tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī[^267-5] vinayavādī[^267-6] nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti kālena sāpadesaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ. Evaṃ kho Cunda catubbidhaṃ vācāya soceyyaṃ hoti. Kathañ ca Cunda tividhaṃ manasā soceyyaṃ hoti?

10. Idha Cunda ekacco anabhijjhālu hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ, taṃ anabhijjhitā[^267-7] hoti ‘aho vata[^267-8] yaṃ[^267-8] parassa, taṃ mama[^267-9] assā'; ti. Avyāpannacitto hoti appaduṭṭhamanasaṅkappo ‘ime sattā averā[^267-10] avyāpajjhā anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantū'; ti. Sammādiṭṭhiko


<!-- PTS Page 268 -->
<a id="page-268"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 268]*

hoti aviparītadassano[^268-1] ‘atthi dinnaṃ atthi yiṭṭhaṃ atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, atthi ayaṃ loko atthi paro[^268-2] loko[^268-2], atthi mātā atthi pitā, atthi sattā opapātikā, atthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā[^268-3] sammāpaṭipannā[^268-4], ye[^268-5] imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti. Evaṃ kho Cunda tividhaṃ manasā soceyyaṃ hoti.

11. Ime kho Cunda dasa kusalakammapathā. Imehi kho Cunda dasahi kusalehi dhammapathehi samannāgato kālass'; eva vuṭṭhahanto[^268-6] 'va[^268-7] sayanamhā paṭhaviṃ ce pi āmasati, suci yeva hoti. No ce pi paṭhaviṃ āmasati, suci yeva hoti. Allāni ce pi gomayāni āmasati, suci yeva hoti. No ce pi allāni gomayāni āmasati, suci yeva hoti. Haritāni ce pi tiṇāni āmasati, suci yeva hoti. No ce pi haritāni tiṇāni āmasati, suci yeva hoti. Aggiṃ ce pi paricarati, suci yeva hoti. No ce pi aggiṃ paricarati, suci yeva hoti. Pañjaliko ce pi ādiccaṃ namassati, suci yeva hoti. No ce pi pañjaliko ādiccaṃ namassati, suci yeva hoti. Sāyatatiyakaṃ ce pi udakaṃ orohati, suci yeva hoti. No ce pi sāyatatiyakaṃ udakaṃ orohati, suci yeva hoti. Taṃ kissa hetu? Ime Cunda dasa kusalakammapathā suci yeva honti[^268-8] sucikaraṇā ca. Imesañ ca pana Cunda dasannaṃ kusalānaṃ kammapathānaṃ samannāgamahetu devā paññāyanti manussā paññāyanti yā vā[^268-9] pan'; aññā pi kāci sugatiyo[^268-10] ti[^268-10].

12. Evaṃ vutte Cundo kammāraputto Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘abhikkantaṃ bhante . . . pe[^268-11] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhante Bhagavā dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan'; ti.


<!-- PTS Page 269 -->
<a id="page-269"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 269]*

CLXXVII.

1. Atha kho Jāṇussoṇi[^269-1] brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi; sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho Jāṇussoṇi brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘mayam assu[^269-2] bho Gotama brāhmaṇā nāma dānāni dema, saddhāni[^269-3] karoma: idaṃ dānaṃ petānaṃ ñātisālohitānaṃ upakappatu, idaṃ dānaṃ petā ñātisālohitā paribhuñjantū'; ti. ‘Kacci taṃ[^269-4] bho Gotama dānaṃ petānaṃ ñātisālohitānaṃ upakappati, kacci te petā ñātisālohitā taṃ dānaṃ pāribhuñjantī'; ti? ‘Ṭhāne kho brāhmaṇa upakappati no aṭṭhāne'; ti. ‘Katamañ[^269-5] ca[^269-5] pana[^269-5] bho Gotama ṭhānaṃ, katamaṃ aṭṭhānan'; ti?

2. Idha brāhmaṇa ekacco pāṇātipātī hoti, adinnādāyī hoti, kāmesu micchācārī hoti, musāvādī hoti, pisunāvāco hoti, pharusāvāco hoti, samphappalāpī hoti, abhijjhālu hoti, vyāpannacitto hoti, micchādiṭṭhiko hoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā nirayaṃ upapajjati[^269-6]. Yo nerayikānaṃ sattānaṃ āhāro, tena so tattha yāpeti, tena so tattha tiṭṭhati. Idaṃ pi[^269-7] kho brāhmaṇa aṭṭhānaṃ, yattha ṭhitassa taṃ dānaṃ na upakappati.

3. Idha pana[^269-8] brāhmaṇa ekacco pāṇātipātī hoti . . . pe[^269-9] . . . micchādiṭṭhiko hoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā tiracchānayoniṃ upapajjati. Yo tiracchānayonikānaṃ sattānaṃ āhāro, tena so tattha yāpeti, tena so tattha tiṭṭhati. Idam pi kho brāhmaṇa aṭṭhānaṃ, yattha ṭhitassa taṃ dānaṃ na upakappati.

4. Idha pana[^269-5] brāhmaṇa ekacco pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti, adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato[^269-10] hoti[^269-10], musāvādā paṭivirato[^269-11] hoti[^269-11], pisunāya[^269-12]


<!-- PTS Page 270 -->
<a id="page-270"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 270]*

vācāya[^270-1] paṭivirato hoti, pharusāya[^270-2] vācāya[^270-2] paṭivirato hoti, samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti, anabhijjhālu hoti, avyāpannacitto hoti, sammādiṭṭhiko hoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā manussānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Yo manussānaṃ āhāro, tena so tattha yāpeti, tena so tattha tiṭṭhati. Idam pi kho brāhmaṇa aṭṭhānaṃ, yattha ṭhitassa taṃ dānaṃ na upakappati.

5. Idha pana[^270-3] brāhmaṇa ekacco pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^270-4] . . . sammādiṭṭhiko hoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Yo devānaṃ āhāro, tena so tattha yāpeti, tena so tattha tiṭṭhati. Idam pi kho brāhmaṇa aṭṭhānaṃ, yattha ṭhitassa taṃ dānaṃ na upakappati.

6. Idha brāhmaṇa ekacco pāṇātipātī hoti . . . pe[^270-5] . . . micchādiṭṭhiko hoti. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā pettivisayaṃ[^270-6] upapajjati. Yo pettivisayikānaṃ[^270-6] sattānaṃ āhāro, tena so tattha yāpeti, tena so tattha tiṭṭhati, yaṃ vā pan'; assa ito anuppavecchanti[^270-7] mittā[^270-8] vā[^270-8] amaccā[^270-8] vā ñātī[^270-9] vā[^270-9] sālohitā[^270-9] vā, tena so tattha yāpeti, tena so tattha tiṭṭhati. Idaṃ[^270-10] kho[^270-11] brāhmaṇa ṭhānaṃ, yattha ṭhitassa taṃ dānaṃ upakappatī ti.

7. ‘Sace[^270-12] pana[^270-12] bho Gotama so peto ñātisālohito taṃ ṭhānaṃ anupapanno[^270-13] hoti, ko taṃ dānaṃ paribhuñjatī' ti? ‘Aññe pi 'ssa brāhmaṇa petā ñātisālohitā taṃ ṭhānaṃ upapannā honti, te taṃ dānaṃ paribhuñjantī'; ti. ‘Sace pana bho Gotama so c'; eva peto ñātisālohito taṃ ṭhānaṃ anupapanno hoti, aññe pi 'ssa petā ñātisālohitā taṃ ṭhānaṃ anupapannā honti, ko taṃ dānaṃ paribhuñjatī'; ti? ‘Aṭṭhānaṃ kho etaṃ brāhmaṇa anavakāso[^270-14], yan taṃ ṭhānaṃ vivittaṃ[^270-15] assa iminā dīghena addhunā, yad idaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 271 -->
<a id="page-271"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 271]*

petehi ñātisālohitehi. Api ca brāhmaṇa dāyako pi anipphalo'; ti[^271-1]. ‘Aṭṭhāne pi bhavaṃ Gotamo parikappaṃ vadatī'[^271-2] ti? ‘Aṭṭhāne pi kho ahaṃ brāhmaṇa parikappaṃ vadāmi. Idha brāhmaṇa ekacco pāṇātipātī hoti, adinnādāyī hoti, kāmesu micchācārī hoti, musāvādī hoti, pisunāvāco hoti, pharusāvāco hoti, samphappalāpī hoti, abhijjhālu hoti, vyāpannacitto hoti, micchādiṭṭhiko hoti. So dātā hoti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ[^271-3] pānaṃ[^271-3] vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā hatthīnaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. So tattha lābhī hoti annassa pānassa mālānānālaṃkārassa[^271-4]. Yaṃ kho brāhmaṇa idha pāṇātipātī adinnādāyī kāmesu micchācārī musāvādī pisunāvāco pharusāvāco samphappalāpī abhijjhālu vyāpannacitto micchādiṭṭhiko, tena so kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā hatthīnaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Yañ ca kho so dātā hoti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ, tena so tattha lābhī hoti annassa pānassa mālānānālaṃkārassa[^271-5]. Idha pana brāhmaṇa ekacco pāṇātipātī hoti[^271-6], adinnādāyī hoti, kāmesu micchācārī hoti, musāvādī hoti, pisunāvāco hoti, pharusāvāco hoti, samphappalāpī hoti, abhijjhālu hoti, vyāpannacitto hoti, micchādiṭṭhiko hoti. So dātā hoti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā assānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati . . . pe[^271-7] . . . gunnaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati . . .[^271-8] kukkurānaṃ[^271-9] sahavyataṃ[^271-9] upapajjati[^271-9]. So tattha lābhī hoti annassa pānassa mālānānālaṃkārassa. Yaṃ kho brāhmaṇa idha pāṇātipātī adinnādāyī kāmesu micchācārī musāvādī pisunāvāco pharusāvāco samphappalāpī abhijjhālu

##### *[PTS Page 272]*

vyāpannacitto micchādiṭṭhiko, tena so kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā kukkurānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Yañ ca kho so dātā hoti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ, tena so tattha lābhī hoti annassa pānassa mālānānālaṃkārassa. Idha[^272-1] brāhmaṇa ekacco pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti[^272-2], adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti, musāvādā paṭivirato hoti, pisunāya[^272-3] vācāya[^272-3] paṭivirato hoti, pharusāya[^272-4] vācāya[^272-4] paṭivirato hoti, samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti, anabhijjhālu hoti, avyāpannacitto hoti, sammādiṭṭhiko hoti. So dātā hoti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā manussānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. So tattha lābhī hoti mānusakānaṃ[^272-5] pañcannaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ. Yaṃ kho brāhmaṇa idha pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti[^272-6], adinnādānā paṭivirato[^272-7] kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato[^272-7] musāvādā paṭivirato[^272-7] pisunāya[^272-3] vācāya[^272-3] paṭivirato[^272-7] pharusāya[^272-4] vācāya[^272-4] paṭivirato[^272-8] samphappalāpā paṭivirato[^272-8] anabhijjhālu[^272-8] avyāpannacitto[^272-8] sammādiṭṭhiko[^272-8], tena so kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā manussānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Yañ ca kho so dātā hoti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ, tena so tattha lābhī hoti mānusakānaṃ[^272-9] pañcannaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ. Idha pana[^272-10] brāhmaṇa ekacco pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^272-11] . . . sammādiṭṭhiko hoti. So dātā hoti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ. So kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. So tattha

lābhī hoti dibbānaṃ pañcannaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ. Yaṃ kho brāhmaṇa idha pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^273-1] . . . sammādiṭṭhiko[^273-2], tena so kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā devānaṃ sahavyataṃ upapajjati. Yañ ca kho so dātā[^273-3] hoti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā annaṃ pānaṃ vatthaṃ yānaṃ mālāgandhavilepanaṃ seyyāvasathapadīpeyyaṃ, tena so tattha lābhī hoti dibbānaṃ pañcannaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ. Api ca brāhmaṇa dāyako pi anipphalo'; ti[^273-4].

8. ‘Acchariyaṃ bho Gotama, abbhutaṃ bho Gotama, yāvañ[^273-5] c'; idaṃ[^273-5] bho Gotama alam eva dānāni dātuṃ, alaṃ saddhāni kātuṃ, yatra hi nāma dāyako pi anipphalo'; ti[^273-4]. ‘Evam etaṃ brāhmaṇa[^273-6], dāyako pi hi[^273-7] brāhmaṇa anipphalo'; ti[^273-4]. ‘Abhikkantaṃ bho Gotama[^273-8] . . . pe[^273-9] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan'; ti. Jāṇussoṇivaggo[^273-10] sattamo[^273-11]. CLXXVIII.

1. Sādhuñ[^273-12] ca vo bhikkhave desissāmi asādhuñ[^273-13] ca, taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 274 -->
<a id="page-274"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 274]*

bhante'; ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Katamañ ca bhikkhave asādhuṃ? Pāṇātipāto adinnādānaṃ kāmesu micchācāro musāvādo pisunā vācā pharusā vācā samphappalāpo abhijjhā vyāpādo micchādiṭṭhi. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave asādhuṃ.

3. Katamañ ca bhikkhave sādhuṃ? Pāṇātipātā veramaṇī adinnādānā veramaṇī kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇī musāvādā veramaṇī pisuṇāya vācāya veramaṇī pharusāya vācāya veramaṇī samphappalāpā veramaṇī anabhijjhā avyāpādo sammādiṭṭhi. Idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave sādhun ti[^274-1]. CLXXIX.

<!-- PTS Page 283 -->

##### *[PTS Page 283]*

CC[^283-1].

1. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave ekacco pāṇātipātī hoti luddo[^283-2] lohitapāṇī hatapahate[^283-3] niviṭṭho adayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtesu. Adinnādāyī hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ gāmagataṃ vā araññagataṃ vā, taṃ[^283-4] adinnaṃ theyyasaṃkhātaṃ ādātā hoti. Kāmesu micchācārī hoti, yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā[^283-5] bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā[^283-6] dhammarakkhitā[^283-7] sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā antamaso mālāguṇaparikkhittā[^283-8] pi, tathārūpāsu cārittaṃ āpajjitā hoti. Musāvādī hoti sabhāgato vā parisagato vā ñātimajjhagato pūgamajjhagato vā rājakulamajjhagato vā abhinīto sakkhipuṭṭho[^283-9] ‘eh'; ambho[^283-10] purisa, yaṃ jānāsi, taṃ vadehī'; ti, so ajānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^283-11] jānāmī'; ti jānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^283-11] na jānāmī'; ti apassaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^283-11] passāmī'; ti passaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^283-11] na passāmī'; ti iti attahetu vā parahetu vā āmisakiñcikkhahetu vā sampajānamusā bhāsitā hoti. Pisunāvāco hoti ito sutvā amutra akkhātā imesaṃ bhedāya amutra vā[^283-12] sutvā imesaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhedāya, iti samaggānaṃ vā[^283-4] bhettā[^283-13] bhinnānaṃ vā[^283-4] anuppadātā vaggārāmo vaggarato vagganandī vaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Pharusāvāco hoti, yā sā vācā aṇḍakā kakkasā parakaṭukā parābhisajjanī kodhasāmantā asamādhisaṃvattanikā, tam[^283-4] tathārūpiṃ[^283-14] vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Samphappalāpī hoti akālavādī abhūtavādī anatthavādī adhammavādī avinayavādī anidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti akālena anapadesaṃ[^283-15] apariyantavatiṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 284 -->
<a id="page-284"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 284]*

Abhijjhālu hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ, taṃ abhijjhitā hoti ‘aho vata[^284-1] yaṃ[^284-1] parassa, taṃ mama assā' ti. Vyāpannacitto hoti paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappo ‘ime sattā haññantu vā bajjhantu[^284-2] vā ucchijjantu vā vinassantu vā mā vā ahesuṃ iti[^284-3] vā'[^284-3] ti. Micchādiṭṭhiko hoti viparītadassano[^284-4] ‘natthi dinnaṃ natthi yiṭṭhaṃ natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, natthi ayaṃ loko natthi paro loko, natthi mātā natthi pitā, natthi sattā opapātikā, natthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipannā, ye imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye[^284-5].

3. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi dasahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave ekacco pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati. Adinnādānaṃ pahāya adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ gāmagataṃ vā araññagataṃ vā, na[^284-6] taṃ adinnaṃ theyyasaṃkhātaṃ ādātā[^284-7] hoti. Kāmesu micchācāraṃ pahāya kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti, yā tā māturakkhitā[^284-8] piturakkhitā[^284-9] bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā[^284-10] dhammarakkhitā[^284-11] sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā antamaso mālāguṇaparikkhittā pi, tathārūpāsu na cārittaṃ āpajjitā hoti. Musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭivirato hoti sabhāgato parisagato vā ñātimajjhagato vā pūgamajjhagato vā rājakulamajjhagato vā abhinīto sakkhipuṭṭho ‘eh'; ambho purisa, yaṃ jānāsi, taṃ vadehī'; ti so ajānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ na jānāmī'; ti jānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ jānāmī'; ti apassaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ na passāmī'; ti passaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ passāmī'; ti iti attahetu vā parahetu vā āmisakiñcikkhahetu vā na sampa-


<!-- PTS Page 285 -->
<a id="page-285"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 285]*

jānamusā bhāsitā hoti. Pisunavācaṃ pahāya pisunāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, na ito sutvā amutra akkhātā imesaṃ bhedāya amutra vā sutvā na imesaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhedāya, iti bhinnānaṃ vā sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā samaggārāmo samaggarato samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Pharusavācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, yā sā vācā nelā kaṇṇasukhā pemaniyā hadayaṅgamā porī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā, tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti, kālena sāpadesaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ. Anabhijjhālu hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ, taṃ anabhijjhitā[^285-1] hoti ‘aho vata[^285-2] yaṃ[^285-2] parassa, taṃ mama assā'; ti. Avyāpannacitto hoti appaduṭṭhamanasaṅkappo ‘ime sattā averā avyāpajjhā anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantū'; ti. Sammādiṭṭhiko hoti aviparītadassano ‘atthi dinnaṃ atthi yiṭṭhaṃ atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, atthi ayaṃ loko atthi paro loko, atthi mātā atthi pitā, atthi sattā opapātikā, atthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipannā, ye imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti. CCI[^285-3].

1. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Idha bhikkhave ekacco pāṇātipātī hoti luddo lohitapāṇī hatapahate niviṭṭho adayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtesu. Adinnādāyī hoti . . . pe[^285-4] . . . kāmesu micchācārī hoti . . . musāvādī[^285-5] hoti . . . pisunāvāco hoti . . . pharusāvāco hoti


<!-- PTS Page 286 -->
<a id="page-286"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 286]*

. . . samphappalāpī hoti . . . abhijjhālu hoti . . . vyāpannacitto hoti . . . micchādiṭṭhiko hoti viparītadassano ‘natthi dinnaṃ natthi[^286-1] yiṭṭhaṃ natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, natthi ayaṃ loko natthi paro loko, natthi mātā natthi pitā, natthi sattā opapātikā, natthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipannā, ye imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye.

3. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi dasahi?

4. Idha bhikkhave ekacco pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho, lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati. Adinnādānaṃ pahāya adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^286-2] . . . kāmesu micchācāraṃ pahāya kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti . . .[^286-3] musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^286-4] . . . pharusavācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti . . . samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti . . . anabhijjhālu hoti . . . avyāpannacitto hoti . . . sammādiṭṭhiko hoti aviparītadassano ‘atthi dinnaṃ atthi[^286-5] yiṭṭhiṃ atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, atthi ayaṃ loko atthi paro loko, atthi mātā atthi pitā, atthi sattā opapātikā, atthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipannā, ye imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti. CCII.

1. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi dasahi?


<!-- PTS Page 287 -->
<a id="page-287"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 287]*

2. Pāṇātipātī hoti . . .[^287-1] adinnādāyī[^287-2] hoti . . . kāmesu micchācārī hoti . . . musāvādī hoti . . . pisunāvāco hoti . . . pharusāvāco hoti . . . samphappalāpī hoti . . . abhijjhālu hoti . . . vyāpannacitto hoti . . . micchādiṭṭhiko hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye.

3. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi dasahi?

4. Pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti . . .[^287-1] adinnādānā[^287-3] paṭivirato hoti . . . kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti . . . musāvādā paṭivirato hoti . . . pisunāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti . . . pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti . . . samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti . . . anabhijjhālu hoti . . . avyāpannacitto hoti . . . sammādiṭṭhiko hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgato mātugāmo yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti. CCIII.

1. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgatā upāsikā[^287-4] yathābhataṃ nikkhittā evaṃ niraye. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Pāṇātipātinī hoti . . . pe[^287-5] . . . micchādiṭṭhikā[^287-6] hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgatā upāsikā[^287-4] yathābhataṃ nikkhittā evaṃ niraye.

3. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgatā upāsikā[^287-4] yathābhataṃ nikkhittā evaṃ sagge. Katamehi dasahi?

4. Pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā hoti . . . pe[^287-5] . . . sammādiṭṭhikā hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgatā upāsikā[^287-4] yathābhataṃ nikkhittā evaṃ sagge ti[^287-7].


<!-- PTS Page 288 -->
<a id="page-288"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 288]*

CCIV.

1. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgatā upāsikā avisāradā[^288-1] agāraṃ ajjhāvasati. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Pāṇātipātinī hoti . . .[^288-2] adinnādāyinī hoti . . . kāmesu micchācārinī hoti . . . musāvādinī hoti . . . pisunāvācā hoti . . . pharusāvācā hoti . . . samphappalāpinī hoti . . . abhijjhālunī hoti . . . vyāpannacittā hoti . . . micchādiṭṭhikā hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgatā upāsikā avisāradā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati.

3. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgatā upāsikā visāradā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati. Katamehi dasahi?

4. Pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā hoti . . .[^288-3] adinnādānā paṭiviratā hoti . . . kāmesu micchācārā paṭiviratā hoti . . . musāvādā paṭiviratā hoti . . . pisunāya vācāya paṭiviratā hoti . . . pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratā hoti . . . samphappalāpā paṭiviratā hoti . . . anabhijjhālunī hoti . . . avyāpannacittā hoti . . . sammādiṭṭhikā hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgatā upāsikā visāradā agāraṃ ajjhāvasatī ti[^288-4]. CCV.

1. Saṃsappaniyapariyāyaṃ[^288-5] vo bhikkhave dhammapariyāyaṃ desissāmi[^288-6], taṃ suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasikarotha, bhāsissāmī ti. ‘Evaṃ bhante'; ti kho te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Katamo ca so[^288-7] bhikkhave saṃsappaniyapariyāyo dhammapariyāyo? Kammassakā bhikkhave sattā kammadāyādā kammayonī kammabandhū kammapaṭisaraṇā, yaṃ kammaṃ karonti kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā, tassa dāyādā bhavanti[^288-8].


<!-- PTS Page 289 -->
<a id="page-289"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 289]*

3. Idha bhikkhave ekacco pāṇātipātī hoti luddo[^289-1] lohitapāṇī hatapahate[^289-2] niviṭṭho adayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtesu. So saṃsappati kāyena, saṃsappati vācāya, saṃsappati manasā. Tassa jimhaṃ kāyakammaṃ hoti, jimhaṃ vacīkammaṃ, jimhaṃ manokammaṃ, jimhā gati, jimh'; upapatti[^289-3]. Jimhagatikassa[^289-4] kho panāhaṃ bhikkhave jimhupapattikassa[^289-5] dvinnaṃ gatīnaṃ aññataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi: ye vā ekantadukkhā nirayā yā vā saṃsappajātikā[^289-6] tiracchānayoni. Katamā ca sā[^289-7] bhikkhave saṃsappajātikā[^289-6] tiracchānayoni? Ahi vicchikā satapadī nakulā[^289-8] biḷārā[^289-9] mūsikā ulūkā ye vā pan'; aññe pi keci tiracchānayonikā sattā manusse disvā saṃsappanti. Iti kho bhikkhave bhūtā[^289-10] bhūtassa upapatti[^289-11] hoti. Yaṃ karoti tena upapajjati, upapannam enaṃ[^289-12] phassā phusanti[^289-13], evam ahaṃ[^289-14] bhikkhave kammadāyādā sattā ti vadāmi.

4. Idha pana bhikkhave ekacco adinnādāyī hoti . . . pe[^289-15] . . . kāmesu micchācārī hoti . . . musāvādī hoti . . . pisunāvāco hoti . . . pharusāvāco hoti . . . samphappalāpī hoti . . . abhijjhālu hoti . . . vyāpannacitto hoti . . . micchādiṭṭhiko hoti viparītadassano[^289-16] ‘natthi dinnaṃ natthi[^289-17] yiṭṭhaṃ natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, natthi ayaṃ loko natthi paro loko, natthi mātā natthi pitā, natthi sattā opapātikā, natthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipannā, ye imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti. So saṃsappati kāyena, saṃsappati vācāya, saṃsappati manasā. Tassa jimhaṃ kāyakammaṃ hoti, jimhaṃ vacīkammaṃ, jimhaṃ manokammaṃ, jimhā gati,


<!-- PTS Page 290 -->
<a id="page-290"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 290]*

jimh'; upapatti. Jimhagatikassa kho panāhaṃ bhikkhave jimhupapattikassa dvinnaṃ gatīnaṃ aññataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi: ye vā ekantadukkhā nirayā yā vā saṃsappajātikā[^290-1] tiracchānayoni. Katamā ca sā bhikkhave saṃsappajātikā[^290-1] tiracchānayoni? Ahi vicchikā satapadī nakulā biḷārā mūsikā ulūkā ye vā pan'; aññe pi keci tiracchānayonikā sattā manusse disvā[^290-2] saṃsappanti. Iti kho bhikkhave bhūtā bhūtassa upapatti hoti. Yaṃ karoti, tena upapajjati, upapannam enaṃ phassā phusanti[^290-3], evam ahaṃ[^290-4] bhikkhave kammadāyādā sattā ti vadāmi[^290-5]. Kammassakā bhikkhave sattā kammadāyādā kammayonī kammabandhū kammapaṭisaraṇā, yaṃ kammaṃ karonti kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā, tassa dāyādā bhavanti.

5. Idha bhikkhave ekacco pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho, lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati. So na saṃsappati kāyena, na saṃsappati vācāya, na saṃsappati manasā. Tassa ujuṃ kāyakammaṃ hoti, ujuṃ vacīkammaṃ, ujuṃ manokammaṃ, uju gati, uj'; upapatti[^290-6]. Ujugatikassa kho panāhaṃ bhikkhave ujupapattikassa dvinnaṃ gatīnaṃ aññataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi: ye vā ekantasukhā saggā[^290-7] yāni vā pana tāni uccākulāni khattiyamahāsālakulāni vā brāhmaṇamahāsālakulāni[^290-8] vā[^290-8] gahapatimahāsālakulāni vā aḍḍhāni mahaddhanāni mahābhogāni pahūtajātarūparajatāni[^290-9] pahūtavittūpakaraṇāni[^290-9] pahūtadhanadhaññāni[^290-9]. Iti kho bhikkhave bhūtā bhūtassa upapatti hoti. Yaṃ karoti, tena upapajjati, upapannam enaṃ[^290-10] phassā phusanti, evam ahaṃ[^290-4] bhikkhave kammadāyādā sattā ti vadāmi.

6. Idha pana bhikkhave ekacco adinnādānaṃ pahāya adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti . . . pe[^290-11] . . . kāmesu micchācāraṃ pahāya kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti . . .


<!-- PTS Page 291 -->
<a id="page-291"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 291]*

musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭivirato hoti . . . pisunavācaṃ pahāya pisunāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti . . . pharusavācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti . . . samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti . . . anabhijjhālu hoti . . . avyāpannacitto hoti . . . sammādiṭṭhiko hoti aviparītadassano[^291-1] ‘atthi dinnaṃ atthi[^291-2] yiṭṭhaṃ atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, atthi ayaṃ loko atthi paro loko, atthi mātā atthi pitā, atthi sattā opapātikā, atthi samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipannā, ye imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti. So na saṃsappati kāyena, na saṃsappati vācāya, na saṃsappati manasā. Tassa ujuṃ kāyakammaṃ hoti, ujuṃ vacīkammaṃ, ujuṃ manokammaṃ, uju gati, uj'; upapatti. Ujugatikassa kho panāhaṃ[^291-3] bhikkhave ujupapattikassa dvinnaṃ gatīnaṃ aññataraṃ gatiṃ vadāmi: ye vā ekantasukhā saggā[^291-4] yāni vā pana tāni uccākulāni khattiyamahāsālakulāni vā brāhmaṇamahāsālakulāni vā gahapatimahāsālakulāni vā aḍḍhāni mahaddhanāni mahābhogāni pahūtajātarūparajatāni[^291-5] pahūtavittūpakaraṇāni[^291-5] pahītadhanadhaññāni[^291-5]. Iti kho bhikkhave bhūtā bhūtassa upapatti hoti. Yaṃ karoti, tena upapajjati, upapannam enaṃ[^291-6] phassā phusanti, evam ahaṃ[^291-7] bhikkhave kammadāyādā sattā ti vadāmi. Kammassakā bhikkhave sattā kammadāyādā kammayonī kammabandhū kammapaṭisaraṇā, yaṃ kammaṃ karonti kalyāṇaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā, tassa dāyādā bhavanti. Ayaṃ kho so[^291-8] bhikkhave saṃsappaniyapariyāyo[^291-9] dhammapariyāyo ti.


<!-- PTS Page 292 -->
<a id="page-292"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 292]*

CCVI.

1. Nāhaṃ bhikkhave sañcetanikānaṃ kammānaṃ katānaṃ upacitānaṃ appaṭisaṃviditvā[^292-1] vyantibhāvaṃ vadāmi, tañ ca kho diṭṭh'; eva dhamme upapajjaṃ[^292-2] vā apare vā pariyāye[^292-3]. Na tvevāhaṃ[^292-4] bhikkhave sañcetanikānaṃ kammānaṃ katānaṃ upacitānaṃ appaṭisaṃviditvā[^292-1] dukkhass'; antakiriyaṃ vadāmi. Tatra bhikkhave tividhā[^292-5] kāyakammantasandosavyāpatti[^292-6] akusalasañcetanikā[^292-7] dukkhudrayā[^292-8] dukkhavipākā hoti, catubbidhā vacīkammantasandosavyāpatti akusalasañcetanikā dukkhudrayā dukkhavipākā hoti, tividhā[^292-5] manokammantasandosavyāpatti akusalasañcetanikā dukkhudrayā dukkhavipākā hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave tividhā kāyakammantasandosavyāpatti akusalasañcetanikā dukkhudrayā dukkhavipākā hoti?

2. Idha bhikkhave ekacco pāṇātipātī hoti luddo[^292-9] lohitapāṇī hatapahate[^292-10] niviṭṭho adayāpanno sabbapāṇābhūtesu. Adinnādāyī hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ gāmagataṃ vā araññagataṃ vā, taṃ[^292-11] adinnaṃ theyyasaṃkhātaṃ ādātā[^292-12] hoti. Kāmesu micchācārī hoti, yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā[^292-13] bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā[^292-14] dhammarakkhitā[^292-15] sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā antamaso mālāguṇaparikkhittā[^292-16] pi, tathārūpāsu cārittaṃ āpajjitā hoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave tividhā kāyakammantasandosavyāpatti akusalasañcetanikā dukkhudrayā dukkhavipākā hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave catubbidhā vacīkammantasandosavyāpatti akusalasañcetanikā dukkhudrayā dukkhavipākā hoti?


<!-- PTS Page 293 -->
<a id="page-293"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 293]*

3 Idha bhikkhave ekacco musāvādī hoti sabhāgato vā parisagato vā ñātimajjhagato vā pūgamajjhagato vā rājakulamajjhagato vā abhinīto sakkhipuṭṭho[^293-1] ‘eh'; ambho[^293-2] purisa, yaṃ jānāsi, taṃ vadehī'; ti so ajānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^293-3] jānāmī'; ti jānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^293-3] na jānāmī'; ti apassaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^293-3] passāmī'; ti passaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^293-3] na passāmī'; ti iti attahetu vā parahetu vā āmisakiñcikkhahetu vā sampajānamusā bhāsitā hoti. Pisunāvāco hoti ito sutvā amutra akkhātā imesaṃ bhedāya, amutra vā sutvā imesaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhedāya, iti samaggānaṃ vā bhettā[^293-4] bhinnānaṃ vā anuppadātā vaggārāmo vaggarato vagganandī vaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Pharusāvāco hoti, yā sā vācā aṇḍakā kakkasā parakaṭukā parābhisajjanī kodhasāmantā asamādhisaṃvattanikā, tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Samphappalāpī hoti akālavādī abhūtavādī anatthavādī adhammavādī avinayavādī, anidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti akālena anapadesaṃ apariyantavatiṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave catubbidhā vacīkammantasandosavyāpatti akusalasañcetanikā dukkhudrayā dukkhavipākā hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave tividhā manokammantasandosavyāpatti akusalasañcetanikā dukkhudrayā dukkhavipākā hoti?

4. Idha bhikkhave ekacco abhijjhālu hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ, taṃ abhijjhitā hoti, ‘aho vata[^293-5] yaṃ[^293-5] parassa, taṃ mama assā'; ti. Vyāpannacitto hoti paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappo ‘ime sattā haññantu vā bajjhantu[^293-6] vā ucchijjantu vā vinassantu vā mā vā ahesuṃ iti[^293-7] vā'[^293-7] ti. Micchādiṭṭhiko hoti viparītadassano[^293-8] ‘natthi dinnaṃ natthi[^293-9] yiṭṭhaṃ natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, natthi ayaṃ loko natthi paro loko, natthi mātā natthi pitā, natthi sattā opapātikā,


<!-- PTS Page 294 -->
<a id="page-294"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 294]*

natthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipannā, ye imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave tividhā manokammantasandosavyāpatti akusalasañcetanikā dukkhudrayā dukkhavipākā hoti.

5. Tividhakāyakammantasandosavyāpatti-akusalasañcetanikāhetu[^294-1] vā bhikkhave sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇa apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjanti, catubbidhavacīkammantasandosavyāpatti-akusalasañcetanikāhetu vā bhikkhave sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjanti, tividhamanokammantasandosavyāpatti-akusalasañcetanikāhetu vā bhikkhave sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjanti.

6. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave apaṇṇako maṇi uddhaṃ khitto yena yen'; eva patiṭṭhāti, suppatiṭṭhitaṃ yeva patiṭṭhāti, evam eva kho bhikkhave tividhakāyakammantasandosavyāpatti-akusalasañcetanikāhetu vā sattā kāyassa[^294-2] bhedā[^294-2] parammaraṇā[^294-2] apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjanti, catubbidhavacīkammantasandosavyāpatti-akusalasañcetanikāhetu vā sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjanti, tividhamanokammantasandosavyāpatti-akusalasañcetanikāhetu vā sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjanti.

7. Nāhaṃ bhikkhave sañcetanikānaṃ kammānaṃ katānaṃ upacitānaṃ appaṭisaṃviditvā[^294-3] vyantibhāvaṃ vadāmi, tañ ca kho diṭṭh'; eva dhamme upapajjaṃ[^294-4] vā apare[^294-5] vā pariyāye. Na tvevāhaṃ[^294-6] bhikkhave sañcetanikānaṃ kammānaṃ katānaṃ upacitānaṃ appaṭisaṃviditvā[^294-3] dukkhass' antakiriyaṃ vadāmi. Tatra bhikkhave tividhā kāyakammantasampatti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti, catubbidhā vacīkammantasampatti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti, tividhā manokammantasam-


<!-- PTS Page 295 -->
<a id="page-295"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 295]*

patti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave tividhā kāyakammantasampatti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti?

8. Idha bhikkhave ekacco pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti nihitadaṇḍo nihitasattho, lajjī dayāpanno sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati[^295-1]. Adinnādānaṃ pahāya adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ gāmagataṃ vā araññagataṃ vā, na[^295-2] taṃ[^295-2] adinnaṃ theyyasaṃkhātaṃ ādātā[^295-3] hoti. Kāmesu micchācāraṃ pahāya kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti, yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā[^295-4] bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā[^295-5] dhammarakkhitā[^295-6] sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā antamaso mālāguṇaparikkhittā[^295-7] pi, tathārūpāsu na[^295-8] cārittaṃ āpajjitā hoti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave tividhā kāyakammantasampatti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave catubbidhā vacīkammantasampatti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti?

9. Idha bhikkhave ekacco musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭivirato hoti sabhāgato vā parisagato vā ñātimajjhagato vā pūgamajjhagato vā rājakulamajjhagato vā abhinīto sakkhipuṭṭho[^295-9] ‘eh'; ambho[^295-10] purisa, yaṃ jānāsi, taṃ vadehī' ti so ajānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^295-11] na jānāmī'; ti jānaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^295-11] jānāmī'; ti apassaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^295-11] na passāmī'; ti passaṃ vā ‘ahaṃ[^295-11] passāmī'; ti iti attahetu vā parahetu vā āmisakiñcikkhahetu vā na sampajānamusā bhāsitā hoti. Pisunavācaṃ pahāya pisunāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, na ito sutvā amutra akkhātā imesaṃ bhedāya amutra vā sutvā na imesaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhedāya, iti bhinnānaṃ vā


<!-- PTS Page 296 -->
<a id="page-296"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 296]*

sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā samaggārāmo samaggarato samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Pharusavācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, yā sā vācā nelā kaṇṇasukhā pemaniyā hadayaṅgamā porī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā, tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī, nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti kālena sāpadesaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave catubbidhā vacīkammantasampatti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave tividhā manokammantasampatti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti?

10. Idha bhikkhave ekacco anabhijjhālu hoti, yan taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ, taṃ anabhijjhitā[^296-1] hoti ‘aho vata[^296-2] yaṃ[^296-2] parassa, taṃ mama assā'; ti. Avyāpannacitto hoti appaduṭṭhamanasaṅkappo ‘ime sattā averā avyāpajjhā anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantū'; ti. Sammādiṭṭhiko hoti aviparītadassano[^296-3] ‘atthi dinnaṃ atthi yiṭṭhaṃ[^296-4] . . . pe[^296-5] . . . ye imañ ca lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī'; ti. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave tividhā manokammantasampatti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti.

11. Tividhakāyakammantasampattikusalasañcetanikāhetu vā bhikkhave sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjanti, catubbidhavacīkammantasampattikusalasañcetanikāhetu vā bhikkhave sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjanti, tividhamanokammantasampattikusalasañcetanikāhetu vā bhikkhave sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjanti.

12. Seyyathā pi bhikkhave apaṇṇako maṇi uddhaṃ khitto yena yen'; eva patiṭṭhāti suppatiṭṭhitaṃ[^296-6] yeva patiṭṭhāti, evam eva kho bhikkhave tividhakāyakammanta-


<!-- PTS Page 297 -->
<a id="page-297"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 297]*

sampattikusalasañcetanikāhetu vā sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjanti, catubbidhavacīkammantasampattikusalasañcetanikāhetu vā sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjanti, tividhamanokammantasampattikusalasañcetanikāhetu vā sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjanti.

13. Nāhaṃ[^297-1] bhikkhave sañcetanikānaṃ kammānaṃ katānaṃ upacitānaṃ appaṭisaṃviditvā[^297-2] vyantibhāvaṃ vadāmi, tañ ca kho diṭṭh'; eva dhamme[^297-3] upapajjaṃ[^297-4] vā apare vā pariyāye. Na[^297-1] tvevāhaṃ bhikkhave sañcetanikānaṃ kammānaṃ katānaṃ upacitānaṃ appaṭisaṃviditvā[^297-2] dukkhass' antakiriyaṃ vadāmī ti[^297-5]. CCVII

1. Nāhaṃ bhikkhave sañcetanikānaṃ kammānaṃ katānaṃ upacitānaṃ appaṭisaṃviditvā[^297-2] vyantibhāvaṃ vadāmi, tañ ca kho diṭṭh'; eva dhamme upapajjaṃ[^297-4] vā apare vā pariyāye. ##### *[PTS Page 299]*

hoti. Kathañ ca bhikkhave tividhā manokammantasampatti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti?  . . .pe[^299-1] . . . Evaṃ kho bhikkhave tividhā manokammantasampatti kusalasañcetanikā sukhudrayā sukhavipākā hoti.

4. Tividhakāyakammantasampattikusalasañcetanikāhetu vā bhikkhave sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjanti, catubbidhavacīkammanta-[^299-2] . . . pe[^299-3] . . . tividhamanokammantasampattikusalasañcetanikāhetu vā bhikkhave sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjanti[^299-4] . . .pe[^299-5] . . . CCVIII

1. Nāhaṃ bhikkhave sañcetanikānaṃ kammānaṃ katānaṃ upacitānaṃ appaṭisaṃviditvā[^299-6] vyantibhāvaṃ vadāmi, tañ ca kho diṭṭh'; eva dhamme upapajjaṃ[^299-7] vā apare[^299-8] vā pariyāye. Na tvevāhaṃ bhikkhave sañcetanikānaṃ kammānaṃ katānaṃ upacitānaṃ appaṭisaṃviditvā[^299-6] dukkhass' antakiriyaṃ vaḍāmi. Sa kho so bhikkhave ariyasāvako evaṃ vigatābhijjho vigatavyāpādo asammūḷho sampajāno patissato mettāsahagatena cetasā ekaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ. Iti uddhaṃ adho tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbatthatāya[^299-9] sabbāvantaṃ lokaṃ mettāsahagatena cetasā vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena pharitvā viharati. So evaṃ pajānāti ‘pubbe kho me idaṃ cittaṃ parittaṃ[^299-10] ahosi[^299-11] abhāvitaṃ, etarahi pana me idaṃ cittaṃ appamāṇaṃ subhāvitaṃ, yaṃ kho pana kiñci pamāṇakataṃ kammaṃ[^299-12], na[^299-12] taṃ[^299-12] tatrāvasissati, na taṃ tatrāvatiṭṭhatī'[^299-13]


<!-- PTS Page 300 -->
<a id="page-300"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 300]*

ti[^300-1]. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha bhikkhave ‘dahara-t-agge[^300-2] ce so[^300-3] ayaṃ[^300-4] kumāro mettācetovimuttiṃ[^300-5] bhāveyya, api nu kho pāpakammaṃ kareyyā'; ti? No h'; etaṃ bhante. ‘Akarontaṃ kho pana pāpakammaṃ api nu kho dukkhaṃ phuseyyā' ti? No h'; etaṃ bhante, akarontaṃ hi[^300-6] bhante pāpakammaṃ kuto dukkhaṃ phusissatī ti[^300-7]?

2. Bhāvetabbā kho panāyaṃ[^300-8] bhikkhave mettācetovimutti itthiyā vā purisena[^300-6] vā[^300-6]. Itthiyā[^300-6] vā[^300-6] bhikkhave purisassa vā nāyaṃ kāyo ādāya gamanīyo, cittantaro ayaṃ bhikkhave macco. So evaṃ pajānāti: Yaṃ kho me idha[^300-9] kiñci pubbe iminā karajakāyena pāpakammaṃ kataṃ, sabban taṃ idha vedanīyam, na taṃ anugaṃ[^300-10] bhavissatī ti. Evaṃ bhāvitā kho bhikkhave mettācetovimutti anāgāmitāya[^300-11] saṃvattati, idha paññ'; assa[^300-12] bhikkhuno uttariṃ[^300-13] vimuttiṃ[^300-14] appaṭivijjhato ti[^300-15].

3. Karuṇāsahagatena cetasā . . . muditāsahagatena cetasā . . . upekhāsahagatena[^300-16] cetasā ekaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ. Iti uddham adho tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbatthatāya[^300-17] sabbāvantaṃ lokaṃ upekhāsahagatena cetasā vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena pharitvā viharati. So evaṃ pajānāti ‘pubbe kho me idaṃ cittaṃ parittaṃ ahosi[^300-18] abhāvitaṃ, etarahi pana me idaṃ cittaṃ appamāṇaṃ subhāvitaṃ, yaṃ kho pana kiñci pamāṇakataṃ


<!-- PTS Page 301 -->
<a id="page-301"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 301]*

kammaṃ, na[^301-1] taṃ tatrāvasissati, na taṃ tatrāvatiṭṭhati. Taṃ kiṃ maññatha bhikkhave ‘dahara-t-agge[^301-2] ce so[^301-3] ayaṃ kumāro upekhācetovimuttiṃ bhāveyya[^301-4], api nu kho pāpakammaṃ kareyyā'; ti? No h'; etaṃ bhante. ‘Akarontaṃ kho pana pāpakammaṃ api nu kho dukkhaṃ phuseyyā' ti? No h'; etaṃ bhante, akarontaṃ hi bhante pāpakammaṃ kuto dukkhaṃ phusissatī[^301-5] ti[^301-6]?

4. Bhāvetabbā kho panāyaṃ bhikkhave upekhācetovimutti itthiyā vā purisena vā. Itthiyā vā bhikkhave purisassa vā nāyaṃ[^301-7] kāyo ādāya gamanīyo, cittantaro ayaṃ bhikkhave macco. So evaṃ pajānāti: Yaṃ kho me idha[^301-8] kiñci pubbe iminā karajakāyena pāpakammaṃ kataṃ, sabban taṃ idha vedanīyaṃ, na taṃ anugaṃ[^301-9] bhavissatī ti. Evaṃ[^301-10] bhāvitā kho bhikkhave upekhācetovimutti anāgāmitāya saṃvattati, idha paññ'; assa bhikkhuno uttariṃ[^301-11] vimuttiṃ appaṭivijjhato ti[^301-12]. CCIX.

1. Atha kho aññataro brāhmaṇo yena Bhagavā ten' upasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammodi, sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. Ekamantaṃ nisinno kho so brāhmaṇo Bhagavantaṃ etad avoca ‘ko nu kho bho Gotama hetu ko paccayo, yena-m-idh'; ekacce sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjantī'; ti? ‘Adhammacariyāvisamacariyāhetu kho brāhmaṇa evam idh' ekacce sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjantī'; ti. ‘Ko pana bho Gotama hetu ko paccayo, yena-m-idh'; ekacce sattā kāyassa


<!-- PTS Page 302 -->
<a id="page-302"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 302]*

bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjantī' ti? ‘Dhammacariyāsamacariyāhetu kho brāhmaṇa evam idh'; ekacce sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjantī'; ti. ‘Na kho ahaṃ imassa bhoto Gotamassa saṃkhittena bhāsitassa vitthārena atthaṃ[^302-1] ājānāmi, sādhu me bhavaṃ Gotamo tathā dhammaṃ desetu, yathāhaṃ imassa bhoto Gotamassa saṃkhittena bhāsitassa vitthārena atthaṃ ājāneyyan'[^302-2] ti. ‘Tena hi brāhmaṇa suṇāhi sādhukaṃ manasikarohi, bhāsissāmī'; ti. ‘Evaṃ bho'; ti kho so brāhmaṇo Bhagavato paccassosi. Bhagavā etad avoca:--

2. Tividhā[^302-3] kho brāhmaṇa kāyena adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hoti, catubbidhā vācāya adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hoti, tividhā manasā adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hoti. Kathañ ca brāhmaṇa tividhā kāyena adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hoti?  . . . pe[^302-4] . . . Evaṃ kho brāhmaṇa tividhā kāyena adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hoti. Kathañ ca brāhmaṇa catubbidhā vācāya adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hoti?  . . . pe[^302-5] . . . Evaṃ kho brāhmaṇa catubbidhā vācāya adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hoti. Kathañ ca brāhmaṇa tividhā manasā adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hoti?  . . . pe[^302-6] . . . Evaṃ kho brāhmaṇa tividhā manasā adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hoti. Evaṃ adhammacariyāvisamacariyāhetu kho brāhmaṇa evam idh'; ekacce sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapajjanti[^302-7].

3. Tividhā kho brāhmaṇa kāyena dhammacariyāsamacariyā hoti, catubbidhā vācāya dhammacariyāsamacariyā hoti, tividhā manasā dhammacariyāsamacariyā hoti. Kathañ ca brāhmaṇa tividhā kāyena dhammacariyāsamacariyā hoti?  . . . pe[^302-8] . . .


<!-- PTS Page 303 -->

##### *[PTS Page 303]*

Evaṃ kho brāhmaṇa tividhā kāyena dhammacariyāsamacariyā hoti[^303-1]. Kathañ ca brāhmaṇa catubbidhā vācāya dhammacariyāsamacariyā hoti?  . . . pe[^303-2] . . . Evaṃ kho brāhmaṇa catubbidhā vācāya dhammacariyāsamacariyā hoti. Kathañ ca brāhmaṇa tividhā manasā dhammacariyāsamacariyā hoti?  . . . pe[^303-3] . . . Evaṃ kho brāhmaṇa tividhā manasā dhammacariyāsamacariyā hoti. Evaṃ dhammacariyāsamacariyāhetu kho brāhmaṇa evam idh'; ekacce sattā kāyassa bhedā parammaraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ lokaṃ upapajjantī ti. Abhikkantaṃ[^303-4] bho[^303-4] Gotama[^303-4] . . .pe[^303-5] . . . upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ Gotamo dhāretu ajja-t-agge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan ti. Karajakāyavaggo[^303-6] paṭhamo[^303-7]. CCX.

1. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi dasahi?

2. Pāṇātipātī hoti, adinnādāyī hoti, kāmesu micchācarī hoti, musāvādī hoti, pisunāvāco hoti, pharusāvāco hoti, samphappalāpī hoti, abhijjhālu hoti, vyāpannacitto hoti, micchādiṭṭhiko hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dasahi dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye.


<!-- PTS Page 304 -->
<a id="page-304"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 304]*

3. Dasahi bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi dasahi?

4. Pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti, adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti, musāvādā paṭivirato hoti, pisunāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti, anabhijjhālu hoti, avyāpannacitto hoti, sammādiṭṭhiko hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti. CCXI.

1. Vīsatiyā bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi vīsatiyā?

2. Attanā ca pāṇātipātī hoti, parañ ca pāṇātipāte samādapeti; attanā ca adinnādāyī hoti, parañ ca adinnādāne samādapeti; attanā ca kāmesu micchācārī hoti, parañ ca kāmesu micchācāre samādapeti; attanā ca musāvādī hoti, parañ ca musāvāde samādapeti; attanā ca pisunāvāco hoti, parañ ca pisunāya vācāya samādapeti; attanā ca pharusāvāco hoti, parañ ca pharusāya vācāya samādapeti; attanā ca samphappalāpī hoti, parañ ca samphappalāpe samādapeti; attanā ca abhijjhālu hoti, parañ ca abhijjhāya samādapeti; attanā ca vyāpannacitto hoti, parañ ca vyāpāde samādapeti; attanā ca micchādiṭṭhiko hoti, parañ ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapeti. Imehi kho bhikkhave vīsatiyā dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye.

3. Vīsatiyā bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi vīsatiyā?

4. Attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca pāṇātipātā veramaṇiyā samādapeti; attanā ca adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca adinnādānā veramaṇiyā samādapeti; attanā ca kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇiyā samādapeti; attanā ca musāvādā paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca musāvādā veramaṇiyā samādapeti; attanā ca pisunāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca pisunāya vācāya veramaṇiyā samādapeti, attanā

<a id="page-305"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 305]*

ca pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca pharusāya vācāya veramaṇiyā samādapeti; attanā ca samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca samphappalāpā veramaṇiyā samādapeti; attanā ca anabhijjhālu hoti, parañ ca anabhijjhāya samādapeti; attanā ca avyāpannacitto hoti, parañ ca avyāpāde samādapeti; attanā ca sammādiṭṭhiko hoti, parañ ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapeti. Imehi kho bhikkhave vīsatiyā dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti. CCXII.

1. Tiṃsāya bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi tiṃsāya?

2. Attanā ca pāṇātipātī hoti, parañ ca pāṇātipāte samādapeti, pāṇātipāte ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca adinnādāyī hoti, parañ ca adinnādāne samādapeti, adinnādāne ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca kāmesu micchācārī hoti, parañ ca kāmesu micchācāre samādapeti, kāmesu micchācāre ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca musāvādī hoti, parañ ca musāvāde samādapeti, musāvāde ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca pisunāvāco hoti, parañ ca pisunāya vācāya samādapeti, pisunāya ca vācāya samanuñño hoti; attanā ca pharusāvāco hoti, parañ ca pharusāya vācāya samādapeti, pharusāya ca vācāya samanuñño hoti; attanā ca samphappalāpī hoti, parañ ca samphappalāpe samādapeti, samphappalāpe ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca abhijjhālu hoti, parañ ca abhijjhāya samādapeti, abhijjhāya ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca vyāpannacitto hoti, parañ ca vyāpāde samādapeti, vyāpāde ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca micchādiṭṭhiko hoti, parañ ca micchādiṭṭhiyā samādapeti, micchādiṭṭhiyā ca samanuñño hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave tiṃsāya dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye[^305-1].

3. Tiṃsāya bhikkhave dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge. Katamehi tiṃsāya?

4. Attanā ca pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca pāṇātipātā veramaṇiyā samādapeti, pāṇātipātā veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca adinnādānā veramaṇiyā samādapeti, adinnādānā veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca kāmesu micchācārā paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇiyā samādapeti, kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca musāvādā paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca musāvādā veramaṇiyā samādapeti, musāvādā veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca pisunāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca pisunāya vācāya veramaṇiyā samādapeti, pisunāya vācāya veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca pharusāya vācāya veramaṇiyā samādapeti, pharusāya vācāya veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti, parañ ca samphappalāpā veramaṇiyā samādapeti, samphappalāpā veramaṇiyā ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca anabhijjhālu hoti, parañ ca anabhijjhāya samādapeti, anabhijjhāya ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca avyāpannacitto hoti, parañ ca avyāpāde samādapeti, avyāpāde ca samanuñño hoti; attanā ca sammādiṭṭhiko hoti, parañ ca sammādiṭṭhiyā samādapeti, sammādiṭṭhiyā ca samanuñño hoti. Imehi kho bhikkhave tiṃsāya dhammehi samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ sagge ti. CCXIII.

1. Cattārīsāya[^306-1] bhikkhave {dhammehi} samannāgato yathābhataṃ nikkhitto evaṃ niraye. Katamehi cattārīsāya?

2. Attanā ca pāṇātipātī hoti, parañ ca pāṇātipāte samādapeti, pāṇātipāte ca samanuñño hoti, pāṇātipātassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; attanā ca adinnādāyī hoti, parañ ca adinnādāne samādapeti, adinnādāne ca samanuñño hoti, adinnādānassa ca vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati; attanā ca kāmesu micchācārī hoti, parañ ca kāmesu micchācāre samādapeti,
